### [1] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TPGC，一种双先验提示初始化解决方案，通过任务先验注入模块和结构先验注入模块显式建模任务先验与结构先验的协同作用，解决了多任务图预训练框架中随机初始化提示导致的提示空间、预训练目标和图结构特征之间对齐不良的问题，在少样本场景下超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务图预训练框架通常使用随机初始化的提示，导致提示空间与预训练目标及图结构特征之间缺乏良好的对齐，这大大削弱了提示表示的任务相关性、结构感知能力和可迁移性。

Method: 提出TPGC双先验提示初始化方案。首先，任务先验注入模块在辅助图上进行简短的同源多任务预训练，使提示初始化继承多个预训练任务的优化偏好；其次，基于任务感知表示，结构先验注入模块从辅助图中提取可迁移的全局结构上下文，通过聚合含结构信息的节点嵌入将其转化为逐层提示向量。

Result: 在覆盖节点和图表分类的6个主流基准上的广泛实验表明，在少样本设置下TPGC相比现有最优基线取得了一致更优的性能，且需要的下游可调参数更少，运行时间更低。

Conclusion: TPGC通过双先验提示初始化方案有效解决了图提示学习中的随机初始化对齐问题，在少样本图学习任务上实现了更优的性能与更高的效率。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [2] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q是一种新的后训练量化(PTQ)范式，通过端到端对齐的代理损失和细粒度动态分块梯度下降来解决现有PTQ方法中信息错位的问题，在LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3模型上W4A16量化下将端到端KL散度相对于最先进方法降低了约49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PTQ方法存在两个主要问题：一是为了保持解析可处理性对全局损失进行了大量近似（丢弃跨通道耦合、池化输出行），导致目标函数被稀释；二是每层量化后冻结的Hessian矩阵无法随损失景观逐列变化而刷新，造成信息错位，限制了量化精度。

Method: REAL-Q提出了一种新型PTQ范式，直接以端到端对齐的全局损失代理函数为目标，而非为解析可处理性妥协。在每128列的分块级别上进行细粒度的动态分块梯度下降以持续修正量化误差，并引入滑动窗口机制实现跨层的平滑过渡，有效缓解误差在网络中的传播累积。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）及Qwen3（0.6B-32B）模型上以W4A16精度进行量化时，REAL-Q相对于最先进的全局引导方法将端到端KL散度降低了最高约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过打破分析可处理性与目标保真度之间的妥协，以细粒度动态优化替代单次闭式求解，实现了更高精度的LLM后训练量化，验证了该方法在大规模语言模型上的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [3] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在AOC-poset结构上实例化Relational Concept Analysis (RCA)时收敛性丢失的问题，分析了收敛性无法保证的原因，提出了确保收敛的条件、数据转换方法，以及一种通过保留已创建的关联属性来保证收敛的变体算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCA基于概念格（concept lattice）的原始定义能保证迭代收敛，但在AOC-poset这一更紧凑、更常用的子结构上实例化时，这一收敛保证会丢失。鉴于AOC-poset在应用中因更小、更易计算而更受青睐，有必要深入研究并解决该场景下的收敛性问题。

Method: 首先详细分析在一般情况下收敛性为何无法保证；然后识别能够确保收敛的特定条件；接着讨论如何通过对数据集进行变换来恢复收敛性；最后提出一种收敛变体，其中已创建的关联属性永不删除，从而以可能引入指向最终结构中不存在概念的属性为代价确保收敛。

Result: 论文阐明了AOC-poset下RCA收敛性丢失的根本原因，给出了可保证收敛的条件及数据集变换策略，并提出了一种保留AOC-poset结构且保证收敛的变体方法（关联属性一旦创建即永久保留）。

Conclusion: 虽然在一般情况下的AOC-poset上RCA不保证收敛，但通过设定条件、数据变换或采用"关联属性永不删除"的变体策略，可以有效恢复收敛性，代价是可能引入指向最终结构中不存在概念的多余属性。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [4] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISTAL框架，一种用于无结构输入的材料性能预测双先验框架，结合自监督成分预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏，在低数据场景下实现成分仅输入的稳健预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性能预测在低数据场景下困难，且高精度模型依赖晶体结构信息，限制其在结构信息有限的早期筛选中使用。因此需要开发一种不依赖结构输入但仍能利用结构先验知识的高性能预测方法。

Method: DISTAL框架采用双先验策略：(1)利用145种成分衍生描述符在大规模虚拟成分空间进行自监督成分预训练，学习可迁移的成分表示；(2)将预训练ALIGNN教师模型的结构知识蒸馏到成分条件的student模型中。最终在统一预测管道中整合显式成分描述符、预训练潜在特征和蒸馏结构特征三种信号。

Result: 在39个基准任务中，融合三种信号的最佳多模态配置在37个任务上优于参考基准，DISTAL在所有特征组合中表现最优。

Conclusion: 成分预训练与结构蒸馏提供了互补的先验信息，为小数据材料信息学中稳健的成分仅输入预测提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [5] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNFT方法，通过内部概率质量重校准来修复扩散模型奖励后训练中出现的模式坍缩（高奖励但低多样性）问题，无需外部信号或接口干预。该方法在保留98.9%-99.0%奖励的同时，将多样性指标提升55%-58.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成器的奖励后训练不可避免地导致概率质量集中于少数奖励偏好的模式，造成模式坍缩并抹除提示内多样性。现有缓解方法依赖外部信号或修改接口（如感知目标增强、参考正则化调整、文本编码器修改），但均无法修复一个已经坍缩的适配器，同时保留已获得的奖励。关键洞察是：在线后训练主要是在预训练继承的能力间重新分配概率质量，而非学习新视觉内容，因此坍缩是抑制而非删除，可以从生成器内部逆转。

Method: ReNFT通过内部概率质量重校检修复高奖励低多样性的适配器。具体包括三步：(1)使用无条件探针优先识别"反中心"提示（anti-hub prompts），在这些提示上可最容易暴露与提示无关的偏置；(2)构建两条策略主导的混合路径，从相同提示和初始噪声生成匹配的反事实提议——一条探索冻结基座方向的被抑制替代方案，另一条暴露后训练后无条件倾向；(3)通过带有自适应翻转守卫的奖励排序分配拉/推角色，实施联合成对的NFT（Noise-Free Tuning）更新完成修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT分别保留了NFT奖励的98.9%和99.0%，同时将DreamSim-Div多样性指标提升了58.8%和55.0%，为外部干预手段提供了互补的替代方案。

Conclusion: ReNFT证明了模式坍缩是可逆的内部概率质量重分配问题，无需外部信号即可从生成器内部修复，在几乎不牺牲奖励的前提下显著恢复多样性，为扩散模型生成器提供了一种自愈式解决方案。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [6] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了注意力层面的上下文学习（ICL）代理指标在被优化后是否仍然可信。研究发现，最大化ICL敏感性的正则化器可使注意力距离逼近几何上限，但行为层面的ICL能力差距几乎为零，且MMLU准确率大幅下降，暴露出Goodhart解离现象。结论是注意力级代理只有在经过行为差距验证后才适合作为训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的上下文学习能力在微调后会被侵蚀，现有诊断方法主要通过检查注意力是否随演示变化而变化来判断上下文敏感性。但该代理度量在被优化后是否仍然可靠是一个未被解答的问题，因此本文旨在检验这一代理指标在被当作优化目标时的可信度。

Method: 本文形式化了In-Context Sensitivity（ICS）指标（匹配与非匹配演示前缀下最后token注意力的平均行距离）和ICL-GAP指标（相同前缀下的行为精度差距）。在Llama-2-7B上设计了四组受控消融实验，包括使用ICS最大化正则化器（armKL）训练，并通过端点统计、随机标签协议和构造性扫描来分析机制。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器将ICS提升至1.413，接近其几何上限的99.5%，但ICL-GAP保持接近零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279，证实了有界注意力代理的Goodhart解离。注意力在高演示间增长为尖锐且近乎不相交的模式，却路由到格式化和演示主体token而非标签。行为门控可部分缓解该效应，而锚定预训练计算的目标能保持高MMLU和中等ICS。

Conclusion: 主要教训是诊断层面的：注意力级ICL代理指标只有在针对行为差距进行验证之后，才应被用作训练目标，否则可能被过优化并产生误导性结果。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [7] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案，与传统的中心化聚合和gossip式去中心化LoRA方法不同，该方法让单个模型token在网络中顺序遍历各节点进行本地更新，消除了全局同步需求，大幅降低通信和计算开销，避免聚合误差，并在非凸目标下提供了严格的收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式LoRA方法存在两大问题：一是依赖中心化聚合的方法通信开销大且存在单点瓶颈；二是gossip式去中心化LoRA需要在多个模型副本间反复同步，同时聚合多个模型更新会引入误差。作者希望克服这些局限，设计一种更高效的分布式LoRA微调方案。

Method: 采用随机游走（random-walk）策略：不维护多个模型副本，而是让单个模型token在网络中遍历，并在每个节点上使用本地微调目标顺序地更新模型。该方法完全避免了全局同步，并通过局部顺序更新来降低通信和计算成本以及消除聚合误差。

Result: 在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上的实验表明，所提出方法在任务性能上与gossip式LoRA相当或更具竞争力，同时显著减少了通信和计算开销。

Conclusion: 随机游走式LoRA微调是一种有效的去中心化微调范式，能够在保持竞争性任务性能的同时大幅降低分布式训练的通信与计算成本，为大规模基础模型的分布式适配提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [8] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型计算包含聚类结构的编码向量的随机概率（最短编码长度）问题，提出了一种递归公式来高效计算归一化常数，将时间复杂度从多项式降到线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MDL原则的数据聚类（如估计最佳聚类数和聚类结构）需要计算包含聚类结构向量的最短编码长度，而直接的NML计算需要多项式时间，效率较低，因此需要寻找更高效的计算方法。

Method: 引入一个递归公式用于高效计算NML模型中的归一化常数，从而避免了多项式时间的直接计算。

Result: 新公式的时间复杂度为线性，相比于之前相对于向量大小和聚类数目的多项式时间复杂度有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该问题被证明是可解的（tractable），通过递归公式实现了线性时间内的归一化常数计算，为基于MDL原则的聚类分析提供了更高效的理论支持。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [9] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在德国、波兰和西班牙三个电力市场（2021-2025年）比较了五个基础模型家族的九种变体（零样本模式）与两个最新的电价预测基准模型的性能，从点预测、概率预测精度以及电池储能套利的经济价值三个维度评估。结果表明，只有TabPFN模型在所有市场所有统计指标上显著优于基准，但统计优势并不等同于经济优势——TabPFN在无限投标和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最好，而Distributional Deep Neural Network基准在风险容忍度较低时更有利可图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（Foundation models）宣称可以在几乎没有任务特定训练的情况下提供准确预测，但尚不清楚它们能否取代专门为电价预测设计的模型，这一关键问题需要实证研究来回答。

Method: 选取五个基础模型家族的九种变体，以零样本模式在德国、波兰、西班牙三个市场的2021-2025年数据上进行评估，与两个最先进电价预测基准模型对比；评估维度包括点预测精度、概率预测精度以及电池储能套利中的经济价值。

Result: 仅TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上一致且显著地优于基准模型；但统计主导并不意味着经济主导——TabPFN在无限投标和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而Distributional Deep Neural Network基准在较低风险容忍度下更具盈利性。

Conclusion: 基础模型无法普遍取代市场专用模型，其价值取决于模型架构以及具体决策问题的性质。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [10] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将特征重要性方法（FIMs）嵌入基于证据权重（WoE）的假设检验框架中，用于量化观测证据对特征重要性假设的支持程度，从而实现对FIMs的原则性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征重要性方法在可解释AI中被广泛使用，但仅靠归因分数往往难以深入了解模型的推理过程，缺乏一种能同时考量先验知识一致性和方法变异性的系统性评估手段。

Method: 将FIMs嵌入基于证据权重（WoE）的假设检验框架，参考假设可来源领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身；并通过理论证明将WoE与归因方差联系起来。

Result: 在LIME和SHAP解释上针对不同参考假设场景进行了实证分析，验证了该框架的适用性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估FIMs提供了一种基于对比和证据视角的补充性工具。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [11] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safin-1基础模型系列，通过记忆路由和状态演化实现"由内而外的安全性"——将安全作为一种模型内在能力而非外部对齐约束。其核心架构MARCH（基于上下文历史的记忆锚点路由）通过内容条件路由维持结构化记忆状态，支持测试时能力状态自适应而不反复修改主骨干网络，从而在共享基础模型上实现受控特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程复杂任务要求基础模型在长时间交互中累积信息、维持内部状态并不断适应。论文主张安全应当是模型本身的内在属性，而非依赖外部防护或事后对齐（如监督微调）的行为约束。现有范式下安全往往作为外加约束，缺乏模型原生计算中的内嵌持久性与适应性。

Method: 提出Safety from Within原则，核心包括：(1) MARCH架构——记忆锚点跨上下文历史路由，维护结构化记忆状态并通过内容条件路由选择性检索历史信息；(2) Safety State安全状态接口——通过测试时状态自适应实现持久能力状态的演进，无需反复修改骨干网络；(3) 路由状态接口将上下文记忆与持久能力自适应统一到模型原生计算中，将记忆从被动历史记录重构为主动维护和演化模型行为的基质。

Result: 在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率等评估上验证了Safin-1的有效性。在下游安全任务上展示了状态自适应机制，取得了显著的安全性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将安全作为"状态原生且可自适应维护的能力"的可行性路径，为安全的模型内在化提供了初步架构探索，但作者明确指出这只是初始探索，实现更广泛愿景仍需大量后续工作。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [12] [Retrieved but not ranked: surface-form bias in structural retrieval, from mathematics to agent trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01556)
*Nabira Rashid,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了"表面形式与语义背离"的嵌入检索性能，涵盖数学竞赛（500查询）与具身智能体轨迹（118查询）两个领域。结果表明：在数学领域严格Hit@1为0%，检索完全以表层词汇锚定而非结构语义；在轨迹领域性能接近随机。LLM重排序可恢复部分差距，但数学恢复部分源于记忆化；下游实验证明求解器准确性仅是截断代理，无提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索评估多在表层形式与语义一致的情况下进行，忽略了真实场景中表面形式被故意"拉开"的情况。作者希望在两个无关领域（数学与轨迹）中统一评估嵌入检索在结构相似但词汇不同条件下的真实能力，以揭示检索锚定的是字面token还是任务结构。

Method: 构建两个基准：MathNet-Retrieve（数学竞赛，500查询/117,088项目）和ALFWorld衍生轨迹（118查询/336轨迹）。在多个"伪装层"下评估多个生产级嵌入器的检索性能，加入词法重排序控制（LLM重排序）与人工评判，并设计配对下游实验（210查询）验证oracle检索与对抗性坏检索的差异。

Result: 数学领域重伪装下Hit@1为0.0%（CI全零），但命中项几乎总在top10内；95.2-99.8%的miss中胜者词法上更接近查询。轨迹领域性能在需不同对象时接近超几何随机、需不同对象和容器时低于随机。词法重排在数学有害、轨迹有益（恢复26-36%差距）。LLM重排恢复5-63%（数学）和43-76%（轨迹）的差距，但数学收益集中于知名竞赛（暗示记忆化）。下游实验中oracle与恶意检索无显著差异（McNemar p=0.678），求解器69.5%准确率基本为已完成答案的截断代理（97-100%）。

Conclusion: 嵌入检索锚定字面token而非任务结构，表层变化的"对抗性"或"偶发性"决定了重排序的方向标记；LLM重排恢复部分能力但混有记忆化成分；该领域基准尚无剩余提升空间，oracle检索本身无法改善下游性能，说明当前评估体系需重新设计。

Abstract: We evaluate embedding retrieval where surface form and meaning are pulled apart on purpose: retrieving items that share underlying structure but not wording, in two unrelated domains under one protocol, competition mathematics (MathNet-Retrieve; 500 queries, 117,088-item corpus) and embodied-agent trajectories (ALFWorld-derived; 118 queries, 336 trajectories). In mathematics the failure is complete: strict Hit@1 at the heaviest disguise tier is 0.0% for both production embedders (bootstrap 95% CI [0.0, 0.0]) while the correct item sits in the top 10 nearly always, and in 95.2 to 99.8% of misses the winner is more lexically similar to the query than the correct answer. In trajectories, where surface variation is incidental, the same models land at or near hypergeometric chance when gold must involve a different object, and below chance for all three embedders once gold must differ in object and receptacle: retrieval anchors on literal tokens, not task structure. A lexical reranker control hurts in mathematics and helps in trajectories (closing 26 to 36% of the gap, CIs excluding zero); its sign reveals whether a benchmark's surface variation is adversarial or incidental. An LLM reranker recovers 5 to 63% of the gap in mathematics and 43 to 76% in trajectories; direction replicates across three judges (all 21 cells positive), but effect sizes, tier profiles, and the outlier judge change with domain (paired differences excluding zero everywhere). Mathematics gains concentrate on well-known competitions (+19.8 points, CI [+6.7, +33.2], one of six cells), so part of the recovery is memorization. In a paired downstream experiment (210 queries, graders at 96 to 99% agreement), oracle retrieval was indistinguishable from adversarially bad retrieval (McNemar p = 0.678); the solver's 69.5% zero-shot accuracy is largely a truncation proxy (97 to 100% on finished answers), leaving no headroom.

</details>


### [13] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不平衡时间序列分类中表征空间的局部几何失效问题，提出了一种轻量级后处理特征增强模块LRG，通过在固定特征提取器与分类头之间注入标准化符号位移和LDA投影残差来修复少数类局部区域的可靠性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多从训练分布、目标函数、logits或阈值等层面干预类别不平衡，但忽略了表征层面的关键问题：当少数类样本减少时，学习到的特征空间在少数类局部区域是否仍然可靠。研究发现少数类样本可能落在稀疏、被多数类主导或混合的特征空间邻域中，即使表征整体保留了有意义的类别结构。

Method: 提出Local Reference Geometry (LRG)模块，它是一个轻量级后处理特征增强模块，插入在固定的特征提取器和分类头之间。LRG仅使用训练特征，测量局部暴露度和类别混合风险，然后对每个固定特征进行增强：加入一个标准化的带符号位移（基于附近训练几何）以及一个LDA投影的残差摘要。

Result: 在UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上的配对原版vs-LRG对比实验显示，LRG对学习表示、预训练表示和固定表示均带来增益，且可与训练层面的干预和后编码器分类器修正相结合。消融实验表明增益来自附加到原始特征的带符号局部残差，而非通用原型距离、亲和特征、标量统计或VLAD风格编码。分析支持局部几何失效假设：少数类邻域在不平衡下越来越多地被多数类暴露，训练局部风险能识别易错区域，LRG收益集中在这些高风险区域。

Conclusion: LRG通过修复不平衡条件下特征空间的局部几何失效来提升少数类分类性能，实验证据支持训练局部几何失效假设，并验证了LRG在不同表征类型和与现有干预手段结合时的有效性。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [14] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FFD是一种硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过全融合内核和top-delta策略打破长上下文解码中的内存墙瓶颈，实现高达11.6倍内核级加速和2.37倍端到端吞吐提升，同时可扩展到256K上下文长度并保持模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型在解码过程中受到内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制二次复杂度的制约，现有的基于元数据度量的方法存在内存开销问题，自适应选择策略则计算效率低下，需要克服这两者之间的固有权衡。

Method: FFD采用硬件-算法协同设计：1) 将选择器和计算器整合为全融合内核，用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引；2) 提出top-delta策略，动态过滤块以实现分布自适应稀疏，无需全局同步；3) 支持训练免费、即插即用的解决方案，并复用扫描结果用于计算。

Result: FFD实现高达11.6倍的内核级加速，可扩展到256K上下文长度，端到端吞吐量提升2.37倍。在RULER和LongBench基准上的实证验证确认其在高稀疏率下仍能保持模型准确性。

Conclusion: FFD通过全融合内核设计和分布自适应稀疏策略，在保持模型精度的同时显著提升长上下文解码性能，为突破长上下文LLM解码的内存墙提供了有效的训练免费解决方案。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [15] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向腐蚀知识综合的领域自适应检索增强生成（RAG）框架，以镁合金腐蚀为示范案例，通过对三种开源语言模型进行微调并结合混合稠密-词汇检索管道，显著提升了生成答案的准确性和忠实度。同时引入Reason Map命题图框架来检测因果关系方向反转和无法支持的推理跳跃，弥补传统事实性指标无法评估机制合理性的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠的预测对及时缓解至关重要。现有机器学习方法虽能准确预测腐蚀速率，但无法解释其潜在机制。在安全关键的材料工程中，不仅需要准确的检索结果，还需要具有机制可辩护性的推理，而现有的事实性指标无法评估这一能力。

Method: 构建了一个领域自适应的RAG框架：首先基于840篇同行评审论文生成3,309个专家验证的问答对，对三种开源语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）进行微调；然后集成混合稠密-词汇检索管道进行检索增强；最后引入Reason Map命题图框架，从生成答案和检索文献中独立构建有向证据图，系统检测因果关系方向反转和无法支持的推理跳跃。

Result: 检索增强使Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度达到0.964，上下文召回率达到0.988。在新发表的文献和内部电化学数据上的盲法外部验证确认了趋势级泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构可跨领域应用，为值得信赖的AI辅助知识综合提供了通用蓝图，不仅适用于腐蚀领域，还可推广至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [16] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECCBench基准测试与评估协议，从效率（FLOPs计算开销）、压缩（可压缩输入的记忆精度/效率）和校准（基于不确定性的弃权行为）三个维度衡量LLM/VLM的记忆能力，超越传统仅基于准确率的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准仅通过长文本或视频的准确率来评估模型记忆能力，但准确率无法反映真实长时程任务中至关重要的其他属性，如计算效率、输入压缩能力以及模型对自身不确定性的判断。

Method: 引入ECCBench基准及评估协议，通过三个轴（ECC）衡量记忆能力：效率（回答记忆问题所需的FLOPs计算量）、压缩（可压缩输入是否被更准确或高效地记忆）、校准（系统是否根据自身不确定性和错误成本选择弃权）。

Result: 实验发现：预训练VLM对文本输入具备记忆压缩能力，但对视频不具备此能力；且在文本和视频上校准均较差。在更广泛的记忆骨干网络中，多种非Transformer架构相比RoPE Transformer实现了更优的压缩-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer，可能成为长时程智能体系统的有用组成部分，提示记忆评估需要超越容量限制去关注效率、压缩与校准等多维指标。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [17] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"天生缺陷，完美进化"机制：通过对AI/ML模型系数施加刻意突变形成模型群，作为抵御环境非平稳性的统计对冲手段，可在变化环境中持续稳定提升性能，并通过四个定理保证遗憾减少，在合成线性回归任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML模型在环境漂移（非平稳）场景下性能会退化，这是真实世界问题的普遍特征。虽然AI/ML早已融合自然选择的理念，但优化过程必然导致模型收敛趋向同质化，难以维持模型多样性来应对环境变化。

Method: 构建一个AI/ML模型群，对模型系数施加有意的突变（偏离最优值），使群体作为统计对冲手段应对非平稳环境。通过四个定理证明该机制能在一般条件下保证遗憾减少，并提出一个简单自适应控制器来调整突变漂移率以匹配环境未知的漂移率。

Result: 在合成线性回归任务中，突变群在约80%的环境变化场景下能提供最优模型；推断综合成功将个体优势转化为集体优势。当突变漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时机制效果最佳。

Conclusion: "天生缺陷"机制与生物进化高度类比，可能是有机发现更接近生物智能的AI形式所缺失的关键要素，可作为AI/ML系统设计中的可泛化原则。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [18] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为精英加权监督微调（EW-SFT）的统一分子优化方法，通过奖励引导的高分分子精英选择来更新模型，无需轨迹级概率计算，可跨多种生成架构和设计任务使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子目标定向优化方法存在两难困境：基于策略梯度的强化学习需要依赖于模型架构和生成过程的轨迹对数概率，难以跨架构复用；而监督微调虽然无需这些机制，但其更新由固定数据集驱动，奖励信息无法进入更新过程。作者希望设计一种既统一又可有效利用奖励的优化方法。

Method: EW-SFT利用奖励来指导高分分子的精英选择（elite selection），然后在选出的精英分子集合上使用模型自身的预训练损失进行更新。消融实验表明奖励信息主要通过精英选择传递，而非集合内的连续加权。该方法仅需打分分子和模型原生损失，因此可统一应用于自回归、掩码扩散和离散流生成器，以及de novo、基序延伸和连接子设计等任务。

Result: 在固定3D形状对齐oracle调用预算下，EW-SFT在两个激酶参考化合物上持续优于相应的原生优化器；在2D相似性oracle的四个留出参考化合物上进一步提升了目标定向优化性能；在无需轨迹级强化学习公式的样本效率基准上达到可比性能。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一种统一的、有效的分子优化方法，适用于多种分子生成器、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle，展示了其广泛的适用性和竞争力。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [19] [Contribution-Aware Bandwidth Allocation for Multimodal Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01406)
*Iason Ofeidis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ModalShare，一种面向多模态分割学习的跨模态分配器。它基于Shapley贡献分数为每个模态分配压缩预算中的保真率，在固定上行带宽下智能分配各模态的压缩资源，相比等比例分配，在CREMA-D和MVSA数据集上分别提升15.4和12.4个百分点准确率，且无需额外上行流量和客户端计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型在边缘设备上训练时，Split Learning需要在每个训练步骤将各模态的smashed activations通过上行链路传输。现有压缩方案对所有模态使用相同的保持率，导致共享预算按激活维度比例分配，而这与各模态对融合预测的实际贡献无关。这种"一刀切"的分配方式浪费了有限的上行带宽资源。

Method: ModalShare的核心思想是将预算分配转化为显式的"跨模态分配"决策。服务器端基于已收到的激活子集计算各模态的Shapley贡献分数，据此为每个模态设定不同的keep-ratio。该方法不增加上行流量、无需客户端额外计算，也不需要对数据流的先验知识。

Result: 在5倍压缩条件下，ModalShare相比等保持率分配在CREMA-D上提升15.4个百分点、在MVSA上提升12.4个百分点的准确率。在三种压缩器、三个数据集和四个预算设置下均表现优异，并证明了现有压缩器在多模态场景下性能不佳，而ModalShare能够恢复这一性能损失。

Conclusion: 跨模态分配是多模态Split Learning中一个被忽视但至关重要的优化维度。通过考虑各模态对融合预测的实际贡献来分配共享压缩预算，而非简单地按激活维度均分，可以在固定上行带宽下显著提升训练精度。ModalShare提供了一种轻量、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly the default option for perception at the network edge, yet they are trained almost entirely in the datacenter, because a client holding several sensor streams cannot host an encoder per modality. Split Learning makes such training feasible by keeping only the first layers on the device, at the cost of an uplink that must carry smashed activations for every modality at every step. Existing compression schemes give each modality the same keep-ratio, so the shared budget is divided in proportion to smashed-activation dimension, a quantity unrelated to how much each modality contributes to the fused prediction. We make that division an explicit decision and call it inter-modality allocation: under a fixed uplink budget, every policy transmits the same expected payload and differs only in how that payload is split across modalities. Our allocator, ModalShare, sets each modality's keep-ratio from a Shapley contribution score that the server computes over coalitions of activations it has already received. Measuring this score adds no uplink traffic and no client-side computation, and needs no prior knowledge of which stream is which. ModalShare improves accuracy over equal keep-ratios by 15.4 and 12.4 percentage points on CREMA-D and MVSA at matched payload in 5x compression, with strong performance across three compressors, three datasets, and four budgets. We show that existing compressors underperform in multimodal settings, with ModalShare recovering what gains are left behind.

</details>


### [20] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WHALE（Weight-Harness Alternating Learning）方法，交替优化模型参数和执行性harness代码，以解决智能体性能受两者共同制约的问题。实验表明WHALE在三个领域（搜索问答、数学推理、国际象棋谜题）中均优于仅调权重、仅调harness以及Fast-Slow Training等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体性能同时依赖模型参数和可执行的harness代码（管理上下文和控制流）。现有联合自适应方法仅优化权重和文本提示，而将更广泛的harness视为固定不动。因此需要一种能够同时优化两者的方法。

Method: WHALE采用交替学习的元算法：一个阶段在当前harness下更新模型（使用在线拒绝采样微调），另一个阶段在更新后的模型下搜索更优的harness（使用Meta-Harness）。切换时机通过固定阶段时长或基于训练信号的自适应耐心规则来控制。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体上，WHALE在best mean@8准确率上比仅调权重、仅调harness及Fast-Slow Training高出4.15-24.38个百分点。同时发现组件瓶颈效应：harness搜索在SearchQA中匹配峰值权重精度但rollout更少，而在数学任务中需权重更新后才提升。

Conclusion: 交替进行小幅权重更新与harness搜索优于分阶段先权重后harness的优化策略，在准确率和rollout成本上均表现更优，表明模型与harness的协同交替优化是提升智能体性能的有效范式。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [21] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向大语言模型的sub-2-bit权重后训练量化框架，将权重量化为三值，并使用具有1:4半结构化稀疏性的显著权重作为残差补偿器，在Qwen3-14B上达到1.7 bits/weight的有效压缩率，同时相比最强三值PTQ基线平均准确率提升16.7%，并实现了比FP16基线快7.2倍的逐token生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型后训练量化方法在低于2比特位宽时往往难以跨模型泛化且精度损失严重。虽然许多方法利用非结构化稀疏性来缓解精度损失，但这牺牲了规则性和GPU友好的执行效率。因此需要一种既能保持硬件高效性又能实现sub-2-bit压缩的量化方法。

Method: QTEA框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 将权重量化为三值(ternary)表示；(2) 使用显著权重作为残差误差补偿器，并将残差分配给选定的列，在显著列内采用半结构化1:4稀疏性以保持硬件效率；(3) 在GPTQ风格的逐列量化中加入列方向的重缩放精化，交替更新每列缩放因子和三值分配以降低重建误差；(4) 识别GPTQ中与顺序相关的误差传播问题，引入误差衰减机制以减弱后期误差累积。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上，QTEA将所有权重压缩至有效1.7 bits/weight，相比最强三值PTQ基线平均准确率提升16.7%，在WikiText和C4上的困惑度分别降低1.40倍和2.61倍。在Llama3-8B上，获得6.6%的准确率提升，同样数据集上困惑度分别降低1.34倍和1.95倍。基于查找表的核实现相比FP16基线实现7.2倍的逐token生成加速。

Conclusion: QTEA通过结合三值量化、1:4半结构化稀疏性残差补偿、列方向重缩放精化和误差衰减机制，有效解决了sub-2-bit PTQ中的精度与硬件效率之间的矛盾。实验表明该方法在多个主流模型上具有良好的泛化能力和显著的加速效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [22] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoLAMP，一种几何感知的潜在自回归生成模型，用于解决微米尺度高度不规则和扭曲结构中的多物理场偏微分方程问题。GeoLAMP采用双编码器图架构同时捕获全局拓扑与细尺度几何特征，在潜在空间中通过带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归预测，并在反应流、热对流和弹性三个复杂几何多物理场数据集上取得最稳定的自回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解多物理场偏微分方程在科学计算中仍是重大挑战，尤其是在能源和化学工程中关键的微米尺度高度复杂扭曲几何结构内。现有方法难以有效处理此类不规则和曲折几何中的物理场建模。

Method: GeoLAMP包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于图表示的双编码器架构，联合捕获全局拓扑和细尺度几何特征，实现从实空间场到紧凑潜在表示的有效转换；(2) 在潜在空间中使用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer建模时间动态，支持稳定且可扩展的分块自回归预测；(3) 灵活的解码器在任意点上重建高分辨率物理场。

Result: GeoLAMP在反应流、热对流和弹性三个复杂几何多物理场基准数据集上，始终实现最稳定的自回归性能，在全滚动预测范围内保持低误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为微米尺度复杂几何中的几何感知PDE学习提供了系统性研究，并通过流匹配框架为潜在自回归PDE建模的分块时间推进提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction. A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [23] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了零样本大型语言模型（LLM）在人口普查块组层面的出行移动性预测能力，使用美国四个大都市区的匿名Cuebiq数据，对比监督学习基线，发现LLM预测准确率明显偏低（最佳0.435 vs 监督模型0.580），且LLM往往依赖粗粒度的先验知识，对受保护群体的预测存在不对称处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度轨迹数据通常具有专有性、受限性和隐私敏感性，而LLM可能作为替代方案生成合理的移动轨迹。然而，LLM能否推断社区层面的聚合移动性尚不清楚，因此需要进行系统评估。

Method: 使用美国四个大都市区的匿名Cuebiq数据，在人口普查块组层面构建点级、轨迹级和时间维度的移动性结果变量，结合社会人口和建成环境预测因子。采用零样本LLM预测，与监督学习基线对比，并通过方向一致性分析检验LLM推断的预测因子效应是否与实证的OLS和Jonckheere-Terpstra趋势一致。

Result: 监督模型平均准确率为0.580，而最佳LLM仅为0.435。空间范围结果的可预测性最强，但LLM与基线之间的差距也最大。方向分析显示，LLM依赖于跨结果和跨城市相似的粗粒度、稳定的预测因子先验，包括对受保护群体预测因子的不对称处理。

Conclusion: LLM能够从城市背景中部分恢复聚合移动模式，但其预测不应被视为具有结构性基础，除非经过实证对齐和潜在偏见的审计。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We evaluate zero-shot LLMs on Census Block Group-level mobility prediction across four U.S. metropolitan areas using anonymized Cuebiq data to construct point-level, trajectory-level, and temporal mobility outcomes, paired with sociodemographic and built-environment predictors. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. Supervised models achieve 0.580 average accuracy, compared with 0.435 for the best LLM, with spatial extent outcomes showing the strongest predictability but also the largest LLM-baseline gaps. Directional analysis shows that LLMs often rely on coarse, stable predictor-level priors that remain similar across outcomes and cities, including asymmetric treatment of protected-group predictors. Overall, LLMs can partially recover aggregate mobility patterns from urban context, but their predictions should not be treated as structurally grounded without auditing empirical alignment and potential bias.

</details>


### [24] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统度量了量化GEMM内核一致性测试套件（容差为单个bfloat16间距）的检测能力。通过在Qwen3-1.7B上对INT8流水线注入9种故障并覆盖8,232个测试单元，发现5种epilogue故障（如缩放精度、双重舍入等）每次都只移动输出不超过一个bfloat16间距，导致容差测试对该类故障几乎完全失明；而违反累加器精确性前提或破坏操作数共享的故障则无一例外地被检测到。进一步发现将所有权重缩放到最近的2的幂次可使CUTLASS与Triton在生成层完全逐位一致，且困惑度影响极小；此前报道的+157%困惑度异常是探测方法缺陷所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化GEMM内核的一致性测试套件旨在验证两个实现在容差范围内是否一致，但此类套件究竟能检测到什么、其检测边界在哪里，此前缺乏系统性度量。理解容差测试的失明区域对安全性验证至关重要。

Method: 在Qwen3-1.7B上向参考INT8流水线注入九种故障，覆盖8,232个层-故障-机制组合单元，测量每种故障引起的输出偏移量及测试套件的检测率；随后在三个模型规模上进行2的幂次缩放干预实验，对比CUTLASS与Triton实现的比特级一致性及困惑度影响，并分离混淆因素。

Result: 五种epilogue故障在5,880个测试单元中输出最多移动一个bfloat16间距，容差测试对其四种完全失明，仅一种在2的幂次权重缩放下被检测；违反累加器前提或破坏操作数共享的故障全部被检测，空故障从不误报。2的幂次缩放使CUTLASS和Triton在全部线性层逐位一致（196/196和252/252对比原缩放的8/196和10/252），三个规模下生成8/8逐字节相同的token序列（对比0/8）。困惑度变化为+0.32%、-0.28%和+0.48%，90%区间在14B上不包含零（达+0.71%和+0.76%）。此前报道的+157%困惑度异常源于探测方法未重新量化权重，99.8%归因于权重-缩放不匹配而非2的幂次约束本身。

Conclusion: 容差套件所能确立的比"可互换性"更窄：它只能验证前提条件成立、操作数被共享、以及差异保持在一个间距内。2的幂次缩放约束不仅是有效的故障暴露手段，也是实用的部署策略，但其在较大模型规模上的困惑度影响需进一步关注。权重-缩放失配是困惑度灾难的真正来源，而非2的幂次量化本身。

Abstract: Conformance suites for quantized GEMM kernels ask whether two implementations agree within a tolerance. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, across 5,880 cells. A tolerance of one spacing is therefore blind to the entire class by construction: four of the five faults are detected by no check in the suite, and the fifth only under power-of-two scales. Faults that violate the accumulator's exactness preconditions, or that break operand sharing, are detected without exception, and a null fault never fires. What a tolerance-based suite of this shape establishes is therefore narrower than interchangeability: that the preconditions hold, that operands are shared, and that differences stay within one spacing. The power-of-two constraint that exposes the one detected fault is also deployable. Requantizing every weight scale to its nearest power of two makes CUTLASS and Triton agree bitwise at every linear layer (196/196 and 252/252, against 8/196 and 10/252 under the checkpoints' own scales) and yields byte-identical generated token sequences at 1.7B, 8B and 14B (8/8 prompts, against 0/8 at all three). Observed perplexity point estimates are +0.32%, -0.28% and +0.48%; the 90% intervals cover zero at the two smaller sizes but not at 14B, reaching +0.71% and +0.76%. A previously reported +157% perplexity for this intervention was an artifact of a probe that rewrote scales without requantizing the weights; separating the effects attributes 99.8% of it to the resulting weight--scale mismatch rather than to the power-of-two constraint itself.

</details>


### [25] [Counterfactual Fragility Certificates: Exposing High-Confidence Brittleness under Structured Evidence Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00366)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC)，一种模型无关的协议级审计证书，用于将每个预测映射为有序的证据失败轨迹，从而识别高置信度但结构性脆弱的个体预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高的测试准确率和良好的整体校准无法揭示个体预测是否在结构上得到其证据的支持。在表格决策系统中，当特征族变得不可用、延迟、有噪声、过期或低信任度时，模型可能仍然保持高置信度，导致失败。现有方法仅提供标量分数或归因图，缺少可重新计算的审计对象来回答在声明证据失败协议下什么轨迹会使预测失去支持。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC)，一个模型无关的协议级审计证书，将每个预测映射为有序证据失败轨迹，通过贪心翻转预算、归一化边际崩溃面积、退化阈值和脆弱性优势得分加以总结。同时提出CFC-FDS（fragility dominance score）作为综合指标，并探索了脆弱性感知正则化和脆性感知温度校正等次要用途。

Result: 在七个表格基准数据集和多种强基线（线性模型、树模型、提升模型、神经网络）上，CFC-FDS以0.915 AUROC识别独立脆性的高置信度案例，较最强非证书评分提升+0.405。在20%审查预算下，CFC-FDS捕获88.9%的脆性高置信度案例，远优于置信度和能量分数的31.8-37.4%。优势在多种扰动、排列重要性、group-SHAP、基线选择、种子方差、预算审查和自然场景字段不可用检查中持续存在。

Conclusion: CFC提供了一种具体可靠性框架，用于揭露普通以分数为中心评估所遗漏的高置信度脆性问题，可作为表格决策系统中预测可靠性审计的实用工具。

Abstract: High test accuracy and good aggregate calibration do not show whether an individual prediction is structurally supported by its evidence. In tabular decision systems, failures often occur when a feature family becomes unavailable, delayed, noisy, stale, or low-trust while the model remains highly confident. Existing calibration, uncertainty, selective-prediction, explanation, and perturbation methods provide scalar scores or attribution maps, but not a recomputable audit object answering: under a declared evidence-failure protocol, what trajectory makes this prediction lose support? We introduce Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC), a model-agnostic protocol-level audit certificate-not a formal robustness certificate-that maps each prediction into an ordered evidence-failure trajectory summarized by greedy flip budget, normalized margin-collapse area, degradation thresholds, and fragility dominance score. Across seven tabular benchmarks and strong linear, tree-based, boosting, and neural baselines, CFC-FDS identifies independently brittle high-confidence cases with 0.915 AUROC, improving over the strongest non-certificate score by +0.405. The advantage persists across perturbation, permutation-importance, group-SHAP, baseline-choice, seed-variance, budgeted-review, and naturalistic field-unavailability checks. Under a 20% review budget, CFC-FDS captures 88.9% of brittle high-confidence cases, compared with 31.8-37.4% for confidence and energy scores. We also evaluate fragility-aware regularization and brittleness-aware temperature correction as secondary uses. CFC provides a concrete reliability framework for exposing high-confidence brittleness missed by ordinary score-centric evaluation.

</details>


### [26] [Adapting Without Gradients: Affine Statistics Transport and What Its Certificate Can Tell You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00374)
*Salim Khazem,Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CASTER，一种无需梯度的测试时适应方法，适用于模型参数必须保持冻结的场景。该方法无需反向传播、优化器状态或存储源特征库，利用源类统计量的判别子空间和仿射变换实现分布迁移，并在28个骨干网络-数据集组合中的27个优于k-NN，同时减少18倍状态开销。文章还引入残差到间隔的可迁移性证书来门控仿射传输，将无条件传输的-3.35点平均效果转化为+1.69点增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试时适应（TTA）假设推理时可以更新模型参数，但这一假设在仅推理加速器、冻结或第三方模型以及内存受限部署场景中不成立。此外，标准基于BatchNorm的TTA在无BatchNorm的架构上可能失效，因此需要一种模型保持完全冻结的适应方法。

Method: CASTER是一种无梯度方法：1）在判别子空间中存储源类统计量；2）从目标批次矩估计类共享的仿射变换；3）在分类前解析地运输源类分布。同时引入经验残差到间隔的可迁移性证书，用于门控仿射传输的开启与否，避免无条件传输在极端小批次场景下造成的性能下降。

Result: 在四个骨干网络和七个数据集上，CASTER在28个设置中的27个优于基于相同冻结特征的k-NN，状态减少中位数18倍。在ImageNet-C上，无条件传输损失21.2 top-1点，但引入证书后门控将-3.35点的平均效果转为+1.69点的增益，且性能在宽阈值范围内保持接近最优。

Conclusion: CASTER为冻结模型部署提供了一种轻量级的适应机制，配合显式的安全信号使用说明。证书具有机制特异性——应用于Tent时仅接受4.3%的更新，保留Tent可获增益的0.6%，表明其对不同机制的适用性有限，需要用户理解其安全信号的适用范围和局限性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) typically assumes that model parameters can be updated at inference time. This assumption is restrictive for inference-only accelerators, frozen or third-party models, and memory-constrained deployments, and standard BatchNorm-based TTA configurations may also become inactive on architectures without BatchNorm. We study adaptation when the learned model must remain frozen. We introduce CASTER, a gradient-free method that stores source class statistics in a discriminative subspace, estimates a class-shared affine transformation from target-batch moments, and analytically transports the source class distributions before classification. CASTER requires no backward pass, optimizer state, or stored source feature bank. Across four backbones and seven datasets, it outperforms k-NN on identical frozen features in 27 of 28 backbone-dataset settings while retaining a median of 18x less state. Affine transport is not always reliable. On ImageNet-C, where batches contain only 64 samples for 1000 classes, unconditional transport loses 21.2 top-1 points. We therefore introduce an empirical residual-to-margin transportability certificate. Across 307 evaluation cells, every transport losing more than 10 points has certificate value above 3.9, although benign and destructive regimes are not perfectly separated. Gating converts an average $-3.35$-point effect of unconditional transport into a +1.69-point gain, and performance remains within 0.3 points of the best threshold over a broad threshold range. Finally, we show that this certificate is mechanism-specific: when applied to Tent, it accepts only $4.3\%$ of updates and preserves 0.6% of Tent's available gain. These results position CASTER as a lightweight adaptation mechanism for frozen-model deployment, together with an explicit account of when its safety signal is informative and when it is not.

</details>


### [27] [Neural means and kernel corrections for operator learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00389)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经网络与精确Matérn核回归（对残差和学习特征进行回归）结合的方法，在两个公开仿真基准（结构力学和OCO-2辐射传输）上验证，在部分场景超越已发表基线，并提供了理论机制解释与不确定性度量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络和高斯过程（核方法）是两类主流仿真器替代模型，各自有优缺点。作者希望探索将两者结合能否在已有公开基准上取得更优性能，并理解两类方法何时互补、何时达到数据本身的性能上限。

Method: 将神经网络预测与对其残差及学习特征进行精确Matérn核回归相组合（堆叠/残差校正）。在结构力学基准和OCO-2辐射传输仿真器上评测，并在不同数据规模下与已发表的多种架构对比。

Result: 结构力学上达到4.55%测试误差（匹配最佳已发布架构），低数据下以5.38%优于已发布的6.49%；OCO-2上在三个光谱波段中的两个优于已发布的高斯过程仿真器；核回归在网络的原始状态上落后十倍，但在网络特征上反超，原因为目标在固定有效维度下原生空间范数降低约四十倍。所有架构的残差相关性高于0.86，共享成分在多样性和样本量上平坦，表明已发表的性能平台是数据本身属性。

Conclusion: 神经网络与核方法的组合在多个基准上有效且可解释；两者性能趋同时数据本身构成瓶颈；二阶矩恒等式可预测堆叠效果，最优恢复证书和分布无关覆盖带是唯一通过测试的不确定性信号。

Abstract: We combine neural network means with exact Matérn kernel regressions of their residuals and of their learned features, and evaluate the pairing on two public emulation problems with published baselines: the structural-mechanics benchmark of de Hoop et al. and the OCO-2 radiative-transfer emulator of Lamminpää et al. On structural mechanics the combination reaches 4.55% test error, matching the best published architecture, and 5.38% against a published 6.49% in the low-data regime. On OCO-2 it improves on the published Gaussian-process emulator on that problem's own test points, outright on two of the three spectral bands; the same kernel that trails the network tenfold on the raw state overtakes it on the network's features, and we measure why (the target's squared native-space norm drops about fortyfold at fixed effective dimension) and prove the mechanism. Where the two families tie instead, the residuals of every architecture we train correlate above 0.86 and their shared component is flat in diversity and sample size, which reads the published plateau as a property of the data. Supporting results include a second-moment identity that predicts stacking outcomes from measured correlations, an optimal-recovery certificate, and a distribution-free coverage band, the only uncertainty signal that survives our tests.

</details>


### [28] [A Multi-Branch Feature Fusion Approach for Health Misinformation Detection and Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00403)
*Mkululi Sikosana,Sean Maudsley-Barton,Oluwaseun Ajao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多分支融合框架，结合心理学理论（ELM和TPB）、Transformer语义、修辞线索和立场表示，用于检测和刻画在线社交网络中健康错误信息的传播，并引入可解释的认知传播评分（CPS）支持扩散风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中健康错误信息的传播日益严重，现有检测方法多依赖纯语义模型，缺乏对认知心理学机制和传播风险的可解释建模；此外，当真实互动数据不完整或不可用时，需要一种基于文本线索的辅助评分来支持传播推理。

Method: 构建一个融合Transformer语义嵌入、修辞线索、立场表示及心理动机代理特征的统一多任务架构，基于ELM和TPB理论设计；并提出从文本中提取的CPS（认知传播评分），综合论证复杂性、情绪强度及内容病毒式传播潜力。

Result: 在Constraint、COVID-19_FNIR和Monkeypox三个基准数据集上取得强分类性能，COVID-19_FNIR上ROC-AUC高达0.9999；Monkeypox上传播排序与真实互动数据的Spearman相关系数为0.9952，COVID-19_FNIR上在代理监督下为0.9954；融合模型在Constraint和COVID-19_FNIR上优于文献基线，消融实验显示心理和修辞分支提供互补增益。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将认知理论与神经建模相结合，提升了错误信息检测的透明度和可扩展性；未来需要在真实场景中用人类中心化的传播判断进一步验证CPS的有效性，Monkeypox数据集的挑战性也反映了领域与信号特异性差异。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-branch fusion framework for detecting and characterising the propagation of health misinformation in online social networks (OSNs). Grounded in the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM) and the Theory of Planned Behaviour (TPB), the model fuses transformer-based semantics with rhetorical cues, stance representations, and psychologically motivated proxies in a unified multi-task architecture. In addition to binary classification, we introduce the Cognitive Propagation Score (CPS), an interpretable post-hoc auxiliary score computed from psychologically motivated, text-derived cues capturing argument complexity, emotional intensity, and content-derived virality potential, to support diffusion-risk reasoning when engagement ground truth is incomplete or unavailable. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, Constraint, COVID--19\_FNIR, and Monkeypox, show strong classification performance, achieving ROC--AUC up to 0.9999 on COVID--19\_FNIR, while propagation-oriented ranking achieves near-perfect agreement when engagement-derived supervision is available (Monkeypox, Spearman's $ρ= 0.9952$) and similarly high ranking alignment under proxy-based supervision on COVID--19\_FNIR ($ρ= 0.9954$). Compared with representative literature baselines, the fusion model improves detection on Constraint and COVID--19\_FNIR, while Monkeypox remains more challenging, reflecting domain- and signal-specific differences. Ablation analysis further indicates that psychological and rhetorical branches provide complementary gains beyond semantic embeddings. Overall, the framework bridges cognitive theory and neural modelling to improve transparency and to support scalable misinformation monitoring, with future work required to validate CPS against human-centred diffusion judgements.

</details>
