Conclusion: 边围长描述符在回归任务上表现出色且无需预设子结构大小，但其表达能力存在根本性局限：当图的边在最短环结构意义下均质时，该描述符退化为常数，任何基于它的模型都无法超越1-WL的判别能力。这一局限在BREC基准中被严格验证。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNN) based on message passing are provably no more powerful than the one-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman colour-refinement test (1-WL): two graphs it cannot tell apart receive identical representations, however deep or wide the network. A common remedy augments node or edge features with precomputed structural descriptors, most often counts of a fixed small subgraph such as triangles or longer cycles, but such counts require committing in advance to the size of the substructure counted, a choice usually made blind to the data. We study a descriptor that avoids this choice. The edge-girth of an edge is the length of a shortest cycle through it, and its multiplicity is the number of such shortest cycles; together they form a per-edge invariant that reports cycles of arbitrary length, computable exactly by a single breadth-first search per edge. Injected into a gated message-passing architecture, EGAGNN, it reaches a test MAE a factor three below the closest gated comparator on the ZINC-12k regression benchmark at 104k parameters; against bounded cycle-counting descriptors under the same architecture, it matches only a dictionary counting cycles up to length eight, using twice as many channels, while a dictionary capped at length four performs no better than no structural information at all. On graph discrimination we prove a matching limitation: on graphs where every edge sees the same number of shortest cycles of the same length, the descriptor becomes constant and any model built on it collapses back to the 1-WL bound. This holds without exception across all 400 pairs of the BREC benchmark: not one of the 90 such pairs is distinguished.

</details>


### [101] [Diffusion as a Training Curriculum for Timestep-Free Iterative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01449)
*Mariia Drozdova,Aidan Sirbu,Pietro Miotti,Robert Obryk,Mayalen Etcheverry,Eyvind Niklasson,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将扩散模型与递归推理器结合的"任意时求解器"，通过为去噪器添加持久隐藏状态并移除时间步条件，实现了一个可任意深度运行的共享更新，在Sudoku-Extreme和Maze-Unique上分别达到99.90%和98.93%的精确求解率。令人惊讶的是，推理时无需渐进去噪，只需在每一步用新高斯噪声替换非线索变量即可保持近完美求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和递归推理器都是迭代式的，但它们在迭代之间传递信息的方式不同。本文希望探究将两者结合的可能性——即扩散去噪器能否作为一种通用的推理迭代机制，从而通过增加推理深度持续提升求解精度。

Method: 在扩散去噪器上添加一个持久隐藏状态（persistent hidden state），并移除其时间步条件（timestep conditioning），使其成为一个可通过单一共享更新运行到任意深度的迭代器。训练时仍使用有序退火腐蚀（ordered annealed corruption），但在推理时保持最大腐蚀程度，即每步用新鲜高斯噪声替换所有非线索变量。

Result: 该方法在Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%的精确求解率，在Maze-Unique上达到98.93%的求解率。精度随推理深度持续提升，远超训练时的展开长度和反向传播窗口。推理时保持最大腐蚀并注入噪声仍能保持近完美求解并能收敛到稳定解。

Conclusion: 推理时不需要渐进去噪——修正训练时的有序退火腐蚀才是关键。这暗示扩散对任意时求解器的主要贡献不在于推理时的采样过程，而在于作为一种去噪训练课程（training curriculum）。该机制无需并行rollout、候选选择或外部验证器，即可通过单一轨迹高效探索解空间。

Abstract: Diffusion models and recursive reasoners are both iterative, but they carry information across iterations differently. We add a persistent hidden state to a diffusion denoiser and remove its timestep conditioning, leaving a single shared update that can be run to arbitrary depth. The result is an anytime solver: accuracy keeps improving with inference depth far beyond the rollout lengths and backpropagation window used in training, reaching 99.90% exact solve on Sudoku-Extreme. We also obtain 98.93% solve rate on Maze-Unique. Surprisingly, progressive denoising is unnecessary at inference: holding corruption at its maximum by replacing every non-clue variable with fresh Gaussian noise at each step retains near-perfect solving and converges to stable solutions. This simple noise-injection mechanism enables a single trajectory to efficiently explore the solution space and settle on the correct answer without parallel rollouts, candidate selection, or external verifiers required by prior reasoning models. Nonetheless, ordered annealed corruption remains critical during training, which suggests that diffusion's primary contribution to our anytime solver is not a sampling procedure at inference, but a denoising training curriculum.

</details>


### [102] [Rethinking Learnability in Offline Data-driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01493)
*Chao Qian,Chen-Guang Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法依赖可学习性"概念，解决了离线数据驱动优化中PAC学习性不足的问题，并基于此提出轨迹学习框架UGTL，在Design-Bench五个任务上取得最佳聚合平均排名（3.1/25）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据驱动优化受到广泛关注，但一个根本问题悬而未决：何种可学习性足以支撑离线优化？已有理论表明PAC学习性不足，因为即使大部分区域学习良好，最优区域仍可能未被充分学习。

Method: 提出算法依赖可学习性概念：仅要求优化器轨迹上的准确性。证明其值查询形式对子模最大化的贪心和局部搜索等离散设定充分，一阶模拟对凸最小化的投影梯度下降充分。基于此形式化轨迹学习框架（轨迹构建、轨迹建模、候选生成），并提出UGTL方法：构建局部连贯的改进轨迹，用条件扩散建模，选择多样化候选集。

Result: 在Design-Bench的5个任务上，UGTL在25种方法中达到最佳聚合平均排名3.1/25。受控轨迹分析和跨架构替换实验确认轨迹构建对性能提升起重要作用。

Conclusion: 算法依赖可学习性为离线优化的理论可行性提供了新基础，轨迹学习框架及UGTL方法展现了理论与算法的有效结合。

Abstract: Black-Box Optimization (BBO) has found broad applications, but evolutionary algorithms and Bayesian optimization face efficiency challenges as real-world BBO problems grow increasingly complex. Data-driven optimization improves the efficiency of BBO algorithms by learning from data. Offline data-driven optimization seeks high-quality solutions using only a fixed set of previous evaluations, attracting substantial attention because it requires no additional online evaluations. Many offline optimization methods have been proposed, but a fundamental question remains unanswered: what learnability is sufficient for offline optimization? Prior theoretical studies show that Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learnability is insufficient, as the optimal region may remain poorly learned even when most regions are well learned. In this paper, we propose algorithm-dependent learnability, which requires accuracy only on the optimizer's trajectory. We prove that its value-query form is sufficient for representative discrete settings, including greedy and local search for submodular maximization, while its first-order analogue is sufficient for projected gradient descent on convex minimization. Motivated by this notion, we formalize a trajectory-learning framework comprising trajectory construction, trajectory modeling, and candidate generation, and analyze existing trajectory-based methods under it. We further propose Uncertainty-aware Gradient-guided Trajectory Learning (UGTL), which constructs locally coherent improvement trajectories reflecting plausible search paths, models them with conditional diffusion, and selects a diverse candidate set. On five Design-Bench tasks, UGTL achieves the best aggregate mean rank, $3.1/25$, among 25 methods. Controlled trajectory analyses and cross-architecture replacements confirm that our trajectory construction plays a significant role in the improvement.

</details>


### [103] [Optimizing Byzantine Node Placement in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01495)
*Edoardo Gabrielli,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出拜占庭放置影响（BPI）作为度量指标，将拜占庭节点在去中心化联邦学习通信图中的放置位置视为对抗性决策，通过量化诚实节点对拜占庭源的累积暴露程度，开发优化算法来评估和识别最具破坏性的攻击者放置配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的安全评估主要关注拜占庭参与者的行为方式，却忽略了哪些参与者被攻破这一关键维度。由于聚合过程分布在通信图上，拜占庭节点的放置位置决定了恶意影响在网络中的传播模式。基于节点中心性启发式的放置标准无法充分刻画加权多跳传播和节点间交互效应。

Method: 将拜占庭放置建模为显式的对抗决策，在固定攻破预算下选择最大化对诚实节点有限时间影响的参与者集合。提出拜占庭放置影响（BPI），一种基于实际八卦（gossip）动力学推导的集合级度量，量化训练过程中诚实节点对拜占庭源的累积暴露。开发高效优化BPI的算法，并评估模型中毒和木马攻击场景。

Result: 在六种异质图族上评估，BPI引导的放置策略在不同网络结构下一致地识别出高破坏性配置，并在通过拜占庭鲁棒聚合放宽线性八卦假设后仍然有效。

Conclusion: 拜占庭放置是DFL威胁模型和鲁棒性评估中被低估但至关重要的维度，本文提出的BPI为该维度提供了有效的量化与优化框架，超越传统中心性启发式方法。

Abstract: Security evaluations of decentralized federated learning (DFL) typically focus on how Byzantine participants behave, while largely overlooking which participants are compromised. Yet, because aggregation is distributed over a communication graph, the placement of Byzantine nodes determines how malicious influence propagates through the network. We therefore treat Byzantine placement as an explicit adversarial decision and formulate the attacker's objective as selecting, under a fixed compromise budget, the set of participants that maximizes its finite-time impact on honest nodes. To approximate this objective without executing the learning process for every candidate placement, we introduce Byzantine Placement Influence (BPI), a set-level measure derived from the actual gossip dynamics that quantifies the cumulative exposure of honest nodes to Byzantine sources over the training horizon. Unlike placement criteria based on node centrality heuristics, BPI directly accounts for weighted multi-hop propagation and interactions among compromised nodes. We develop efficient algorithms for optimizing BPI and evaluate them across six heterogeneous graph families, untargeted model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. BPI-guided placements consistently identify highly damaging configurations across different network structures and remain effective when the linear gossip assumption is relaxed through Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that Byzantine placement is a critical but under-modeled dimension of DFL threat models and robustness evaluations.

</details>


### [104] [LatentPress: Context Compression Beyond Text and Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01507)
*Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentPress，一种将对话历史和长文档压缩为连续记忆token（软token）的方法，冻结解码器直接通过输入嵌入接口读取，无需推理时的文本重建。在LongMemEval上达到7.70倍压缩下0.504的准确率（优于未压缩的0.490），写作速度比文本摘要快约一个数量级，阅读速度比原始上下文快5-9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩上下文以人类可读文本或渲染图像形式存在，即使消费方是语言模型也需解码。本文旨在探索第三种表示——直接被冻结解码器读取的连续记忆token，无需推理时文本重建，以提升压缩效率与速度。

Method: LatentPress使用一个小型writer（仅4.2M-26.2M参数，约为解码器的0.1%）将上下文压缩4-16倍为连续软token，解码器冻结并通过输入嵌入接口直接读取这些token。仅需训练adapter，无需文本重建。

Result: LongMemEval上7.70x压缩达到0.504准确率（未压缩为0.490），超越文本摘要（0.184）和OCR压缩（0.426-0.312）。LongBench-QA上4-8x压缩匹配或超越原始上下文读取，16x压缩略有落后。写作43ms/会话，比文本摘要或OCR快约一个数量级；阅读比原始上下文或缓存OCR快5-9倍。两种迁移设置均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 直接软token可作为超越文本和视觉的实用机器面向上下文接口，实现高效、快速的上下文压缩与读取，且具备跨领域迁移能力。

Abstract: Compressed context is usually carried as human-readable text or as rendered images that must be decoded, even when its consumer is a language model. We introduce LatentPress, which writes conversational histories and long documents into a third representation: continuous memory tokens that a frozen decoder reads directly through its input-embedding interface, with no text reconstruction at inference. A small reader-matched writer compresses $4$-$16\times$ while training only an adapter (4.2M-26.2M parameters, $\sim\!0.1\%$ of the decoder). On LongMemEval, LatentPress reaches $0.504$ accuracy at $7.70\times$ compression versus $0.490$ for uncompressed evidence, outperforming text summaries (0.184) and OCR-based compression (0.426 to 0.312). On LongBench-QA, in-domain writers match or exceed raw-context reading at $4$-$8\times$ compression, while $16\times$ trails raw. Writing takes 43ms per conversation, roughly an order of magnitude faster than text summarization or OCR reconstruction, and reading is $5$-$9\times$ faster than raw context or cached OCR. We validate the interface under two transfer settings, zero-shot from UltraChat to LongMemEval memory QA and from LongMemEval-derived QA to unseen LongBench document domains, establishing direct soft tokens as a practical machine-facing context interface beyond text and vision. The implementation of the experiments could be found at: https://github.com/xuyd16ai/context_softtoken_compress .

</details>


### [105] [Quantum Sparse Autoencoders for Q-Matrix Estimation in Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01537)
*Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Bowen Guo,Xiang Li,Yu Bao,Yingfeng Wang,Tianming Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将量子机器学习应用于认知诊断中的Q矩阵估计，提出量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE），将学生的二进制响应向量编码到量子电路中进行稀疏压缩学习。在60个模拟数据集和9个真实数据集上与经典自编码器对比，发现QSAE虽在部分模拟条件下精度略低，但在49/60条件下方差更低、稳定性更强，且9个真实数据集中6个表现更优。核心贡献在于QML在该任务中带来的不是普适精度提升，而是更强的鲁棒性和对潜在结构复杂性的探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q矩阵在认知诊断中至关重要，但数据驱动的Q矩阵估计在技能高度相关及真实响应模式偏离理想生成假设时仍具挑战性。传统方法在上述场景下存在鲁棒性不足的局限，需要探索新的建模范式。量子机器学习的引入，为处理高维、复杂潜在结构的Q矩阵估计提供了全新思路，这是首次将QML与该领域结合。

Method: 提出量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE），该框架将学生的二进制响应向量通过编码器嵌入量子电路，通过量子编码压缩为稀疏潜在表示，再将该表示映射到Q矩阵。实验设计上，与经典自编码器（CAE）在60个模拟条件与9个真实教育评估数据集上进行系统对比。

Result: CAE在部分模拟条件下获得更高平均精度，但QSAE在60个模拟条件的49个中展现出更低的重复抽样方差，稳定性优势显著。在9个真实数据集中，QSAE在6个数据集上超越CAE。两类方法呈现互补而非替代的关系。

Conclusion: QML在Q矩阵估计中的核心价值不在于全面提升准确性，而在于增强模型对重复样本的鲁棒性，并能更好地探索真实数据中复杂的潜在结构。这一发现为该领域指明了量子算法可行的应用定位——并非替代经典模型的精度追求，而是作为应对复杂真实场景的稳健补充工具。

Abstract: Q-matrices play a central role in cognitive diagnosis within educational data mining (EDM), specifying which latent skills each assessment item requires. Data-driven Q-matrix estimation remains challenging when assessments involve many correlated skills and when real response patterns depart from idealized generative assumptions. We introduce a novel quantum sparse autoencoder (QSAE) for Q-matrix estimation, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first application of quantum machine learning (QML) to cognitive diagnosis. Overall, the QSAE embeds each student's binary response vector into a quantum circuit using an encoder, compresses it into a sparse latent representation, and maps that representation to the Q-matrix. We benchmark the QSAE against a classical autoencoder (CAE) across 60 simulated datasets and 9 real-world assessment datasets. The results reveal complementary strengths. Although the CAE partially achieves higher average accuracy under several simulation conditions, the QSAE is substantially more stable across replications, exhibiting lower variance in 49 of the 60 conditions. Moreover, on real assessment data, the QSAE outperforms the CAE on 6 of the 9 datasets. These findings suggest that the principal advancement of QML in this setting is not universal accuracy improvement, but enhanced robustness and capability to explore latent-structure complexity in real datasets.

</details>


### [106] [NashDreamer: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01549)
*Tomáš Holeček,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NashDreamer，一种用于两人零和不完全信息博弈的基于模型的强化学习框架，通过集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM）将环境动态与玩家策略对观测的影响解耦，显著提升训练早期的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）在单智能体领域取得了显著成果，但在竞争性不完全信息博弈（IIG）中的扩展尚未得到充分探索。多智能体环境中对手诱发的非平稳性使学习过程复杂化，且分散式模型学习面临严重的可辨识性障碍，作者论证集中式模型学习是数学上的必然选择。

Method: 提出NashDreamer框架，包含集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM），该模型将环境动态与玩家策略对个体观测的影响进行解耦。框架设计为可使用任意策略梯度算法，并在理想模型假设下继承其收敛到纳什均衡的保证。

Result: 在四个基准博弈游戏上的实证评估表明，NashDreamer在训练早期相较无模型基线显著提升了样本效率。此外，作者通过理论分析揭示了Dreamer系列算法在随机环境中对后验坍缩的脆弱性。

Conclusion: NashDreamer为竞争性不完全信息博弈中的MBRL提供了一个有原理支撑的框架，展示了集中式学习的必要性并验证了其样本效率优势。同时指出Dreamer系列算法在随机环境中的后验坍缩问题仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable results in single-agent domains, yet its extension to competitive imperfect information games (IIGs) remains underexplored. In multi-agent settings, opponent-induced non-stationarity complicates the learning process, and decentralized model learning faces severe identifiability barriers, which we argue make centralized model learning a mathematical necessity. Building on this analysis, we propose NashDreamer, a principled MBRL framework for two-player zero-sum IIGs. It introduces a centralized Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model (MARSSM) that decouples environment dynamics from the effect of players' strategies on their individual observations. NashDreamer is designed to use arbitrary policy gradient algorithms and inherits their convergence guarantees towards Nash equilibria under an idealized model. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark games demonstrate that NashDreamer substantially improves sample efficiency over model-free baselines early in the training. Finally, we theoretically analyze the architecture's optimization landscape, identifying the vulnerability of the Dreamer family of algorithms to posterior collapse in stochastic environments. We leave it as an open challenge.

</details>


### [107] [A Mathematical Theory of Reusable Neural Bases for Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01550)
*Binshuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出线性可复用神经基座架构（LRNBA），通过将网络块表示为共享神经基座的线性组合，在保持稳定训练的同时大幅提升参数效率、降低内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型AI模型在训练和推理中的内存成本已成为关键瓶颈，亟需提升参数效率并降低内存开销。

Method: 受RNN设计启发，将每个网络块表示为共享神经基座的线性组合，实现高压缩率并保持训练稳定性，可在相同参数预算下构建更宽更深的网络。

Result: 实验表明该方法相比经典架构能达到相当甚至更快的收敛速度和更低的损失，同时保持稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: LRNBA在参数效率和内存成本方面具有显著优势，为构建大规模网络提供了一种高效可行的新范式。

Abstract: As large AI models become increasingly prevalent across a wide range of applications, memory cost has become a critical bottleneck in both training and inference. To mitigate this issue, we introduce the Linear Reusable Neural Bases Architecture (LRNBA), a novel framework aimed at improving parameter efficiency and reducing memory cost. Inspired by recurrent neural network (RNN) designs, the core idea of our approach is to represent each network block as a linear combination of a shared set of neural bases, thereby enjoying highly network compression rate while maintaining stable training. The proposed architecture allows for the construction of significantly wider and deeper networks under the same parameter budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model achieves comparable or even faster convergence and lower loss than classical architectures, while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Gradient-Update Mismatch: Rethinking Conflict-Free Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01558)
*Jing Xiao,Xinhai Chen,Qinglin Wang,Menghan Jia,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li,Jie Liu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度-更新错配（GUM）问题，指出在物理信息神经网络（PINNs）训练中，即使梯度手术构造的方向无冲突，优化器的变换（如动量、自适应缩放、预条件处理、解耦权重衰减）也可能导致应用更新重新产生梯度冲突。作者提出梯度-更新对齐（GUA）方法，将优化器更新投影到无冲突锥上，并调整优化器内部状态以匹配对齐后的更新，实验显示GUM冲突率高达86.3%，GUA能使L2误差相对降低最高98.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs训练需要联合优化物理残差和初始/边界条件损失，这些损失项常产生冲突的梯度。虽然梯度手术方法能构造无冲突方向，但现有方法忽略了优化器变换后更新方向可能重新引入冲突的问题，缺乏对这一现象的正式分析和解决方案。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义梯度-更新错配（GUM），证明优化器的历史状态、自适应缩放、预条件处理或解耦权重衰减机制会使无冲突方向a_t在变换为优化器提案u_t后不再保证无冲突。随后提出梯度-更新对齐（GUA）：将u_t投影到无冲突锥C_t上得到对齐更新p_t并应用；当优化器维护内部状态时，从应用的更新反向重建目标并相应调整内部状态。

Result: 实验表明GUM在动量、自适应和基于曲率的优化器中广泛存在，冲突率最高达86.3%。GUA在所有PINN设置中实现无冲突的应用更新，并一致性地改进各种梯度手术方法，在个别设置中相对L2误差降低高达98.2%。

Conclusion: 梯度手术在优化器变换层面并未充分解决梯度冲突问题，GUM是实际训练中的重大问题。GUA通过在优化器更新层面而非仅梯度构造层面保证无冲突性，为PINNs训练提供了一种有效的通用改进方案。

Abstract: Training Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) requires jointly optimizing physics residual and initial/boundary condition loss terms, which often induce conflicting gradients. Gradient surgery methods mitigate this issue by constructing directions from loss-specific gradients to reduce conflict before optimizer transformation. However, even when the constructed direction is conflict-free, this property may not be preserved after optimizer transformation. Let $a_t$ denote the direction constructed by gradient surgery, $u_t$ the optimizer proposal, and $\mathcal{C}_t$ the conflict-free cone induced by the loss-specific gradients. We show that modern optimizers can transform $a_t$ through mechanisms such as historical state, adaptive scaling, preconditioning, or decoupled weight decay, so $a_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$ does not generally imply $u_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$. We refer to this optimizer-induced discrepancy in conflict-freeness between $a_t$ and $u_t$ as Gradient-Update Mismatch (GUM). Accordingly, we propose Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA), which projects $u_t$ onto $\mathcal{C}_t$ to obtain the aligned update $p_t$ and applies $p_t$ to the parameters. When the optimizer maintains internal state, GUA further adjusts this state toward targets reconstructed from the applied update. We conduct extensive experiments and find that GUM is widespread across momentum, adaptive, and curvature-based optimizers, with conflict rates reaching up to 86.3%. Across all PINN settings, GUA achieves conflict-free applied updates and consistently improves various gradient surgery methods, reducing the relative $L_2$ error by up to 98.2% in individual settings. Data and code are available at https://github.com/JingXiao10/GUA.

</details>


### [109] [The Structure of Quantization Damage in LLMs: Why the Next Bit Should Be Spent Globally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01587)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果混合精度干预实验探究了后训练量化（PTQ）对LLM精度损失的分布规律，发现量化损伤是弥散分布的而非集中在特定层或任务回路中，且在全球精度预算下，全局细粒度量化优于选择性修复关键层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ被广泛用于降低LLM服务成本，但其精度损失不均匀且通常需逐模型调整。作者希望弄清量化损伤发生在何处，以及如何分配少量额外精度预算以达到最优效果。

Method: 在9个开源模型（4个架构家族）上，以因果混合精度干预为基准（依次将每层提升至8位并测量精度恢复），测试了3个假设：损伤存在于任务回路、模型计算处或权重统计中。同时对比全局细粒度量化与局部修复高恢复层的策略在匹配精度预算下的表现。

Result: 三个直觉假设均无法预测哪些层能从精度恢复中获益；损伤是弥散的（8/9模型中恢复75%差距需约一半层数，仅Qwen3-8B是集中例外）。在匹配精度预算下，全局细粒度量化对8个group-128兼容模型均优于局部修复（超出21-52点）。次要发现：8位精度近乎无损（剩余损伤受预算限制）；峰值恢复位置在家族内与架构相关但跨家族不相关。

Conclusion: 在该预算设定下，全局细粒度量化优于选择性保护关键层。更重要的是，能关联量化损伤的廉价信号不一定能识别出精度恢复有效的层，这一关系须通过因果干预来验证。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is widely used to reduce the cost of serving large language models (LLMs), but its accuracy cost is uneven and is often tuned per model. We study where quantization damage occurs and how to allocate a small additional precision budget. Using causal mixed-precision intervention as ground truth (raise each layer to 8-bit in turn and measure the accuracy it recovers) across 9 open-weight models in 4 architecture families, we test 3 intuitive hypotheses: that quantization damage lives in task circuits, where the model computes, or in weight statistics. None of them predicts which layers benefit from restored precision. Recovery is instead diffuse: for 8 of 9 models, recovering 75% of the gap takes roughly half the layers; the lone exception, Qwen3-8B, is sharply concentrated. At a matched precision budget, spending it globally on finer quantization granularity beats locally repairing the most recoverable layers for all 8 group-128-compatible models (all but OpenLLaMA, whose width rules out group-128), by 21-52 points, including the concentrated Qwen3-8B. We report 2 secondary findings: the residual is budget-limited (8-bit is near-lossless in our evaluation across RTN, GPTQ, and AWQ), and the location of peak recovery correlates with architecture within a family, though not across families. Within this budget setting, global granularity is a better default than selectively protecting critical layers. More broadly, cheap signals that correlate with quantization damage do not necessarily identify where restoring precision improves accuracy; this must be tested with causal intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [BlockMGARD: Accelerating Adaptive Scientific Data Reduction with Region-of-Interest Error Control on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00205)
*Yanliang Li,Qian Gong,Qing Liu,Jaemoon Lee,Norbert Podhorszki,Scott Klasky,Xin Liang,Jieyang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出BlockMGARD，一种基于GPU的自适应有损压缩器，通过In-cache Block分解、混合层级和ROI误差控制，在压缩/解压吞吐量和压缩比上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据规模不断增长，有损压缩成为降低数据体积的关键手段。基于多级分解的压缩器（如MGARD）虽能实现高压缩比，但难以有效映射到GPU架构上，亟需针对GPU优化的高效压缩方案。

Method: BlockMGARD包含四项核心技术：(1) In-cache Block分解，利用GPU片上内存和常量查找表加速分解过程；(2) 混合层级结构，结合In-cache Block与全局分解以平衡速度与压缩比；(3) 端到端流水线，支持细粒度ROI误差控制以保留特征；(4) 在五个真实数据集上进行全面评估。

Result: 相比MGARD-X，BlockMGARD的压缩和解压吞吐量最高提升4.2倍和9.1倍；在ROI误差控制下，压缩比相比均匀容差基线最高提升8.63倍；在四种GPU上实现接近理想的线性扩展，I/O成本较MGARD-X最多降低1.58倍。

Conclusion: BlockMGARD证明了通过GPU架构友好的块分解与混合层级设计，能够在保持高压缩比的同时大幅提升吞吐量和扩展性，是一种高效实用的GPU有损压缩方案。

Abstract: The growing scale of scientific data makes lossy compression essential for reducing data volume under controllable error. Transformation-based compressors using multilevel decomposition, such as MGARD, achieve strong compression ratios but map poorly to GPU architectures. We propose BlockMGARD, an adaptive, Region-of-Interest (ROI)-supported GPU lossy compressor, with four contributions: (1) an In-cache Block decomposition leveraging GPU on-chip memory and constant lookup tables to accelerate decomposition; (2) a hybrid hierarchy combining In-cache Block and global decomposition to balance speed and compression ratio; (3) an end-to-end pipeline with fine-grained ROI error control for feature preservation; and (4) an evaluation against state-of-the-art methods on five real-world datasets. Compared to MGARD-X, BlockMGARD achieves up to 4.2x and 9.1x higher compression and decompression throughput, and up to 8.63x higher compression ratio than uniform-tolerance baselines under ROI-aware error control. Across four GPUs, BlockMGARD achieves near-ideal linear scaling and up to 1.58x I/O cost reduction over MGARD-X.

</details>


### [111] [Operation-Type-Aware Client Routing for Leader-Based Consensus Datastores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00392)
*Sri Saran Balaji Vellore Rajakumar,James Thompson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于领导者的共识数据库（etcd、ZooKeeper）中的客户端路由策略。提出一种"操作感知"的客户端路由方法：将写操作固定路由到领导者，将读操作分散到健康的读池成员。在3节点etcd集群80/20读写混合下，写P50降低29%、吞吐量提升9%；当跟随者静默退化时，读P99降低64%、写P99降低74%、吞吐量提升89%。同样的规则在ZooKeeper上验证有效，证明该结果源于领导者共识结构的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于领导者的共识数据存储（如etcd、ZooKeeper）面临两个竞争性路由目标：均衡负载与按协议角色匹配路由。写操作必须经过领导者提交，线性化读则需要轻量领导者确认。现有gRPC轮询平衡器将所有操作均匀分发，未能利用协议特性优化路由效率。

Method: 提出操作感知客户端路由策略：将写操作固定路由至领导者节点；将读操作分发给健康的读池成员（所有可正常服务的成员）；通过检测延迟偏移识别静默退化的跟随者并将其从读池中移除。该规则在etcd和ZooKeeper（ZAB协议）两种不同实现上分别验证。

Result: 在3节点etcd集群（80/20读写混合、5次试验）稳态下，写P50降低29%、吞吐量提升9%。跟随者静默退化时，读P99降低64%、写P99降低74%、吞吐量提升89%。同样的路由规则在ZooKeeper上验证结果方向一致。

Conclusion: 操作感知路由策略显著提升领导者共识数据库的性能与故障恢复能力，结果与具体系统实现无关，反映了领导者共识结构的本质特性。发现该策略的核心障碍在于领导者确认往返发生在集群成员之间，客户端只能观测到混合延迟信号，无法直接感知协调成本。

Abstract: Leader-based consensus datastores (etcd, ZooKeeper) face two competing routing goals: spread load evenly across members, and route operations to the member whose protocol role matches the operation. Writes must commit through the leader, so sending them elsewhere adds a forwarding hop. Linearizable reads need only a lightweight leader confirmation before any member can serve them locally. The upstream etcd client uses gRPC's round_robin balancer, distributing reads and writes uniformly across cluster members. An operation-aware client resolves this by pinning writes to the leader and distributing reads across the healthy read pool. In steady state on a 3-node etcd cluster (80/20 read/write mix, 5 trials), this lowers write P50 by 29% and raises throughput by 9%. When a follower degrades silently, the operation-aware client detects the latency shift and removes it from the read pool, cutting read P99 by 64%, write P99 by 74%, and raising throughput by 89%. The same routing rule applied to ZooKeeper (ZAB protocol, different implementation) points in the same direction, showing that the result follows from leader-based consensus structure rather than one system's implementation. The key obstacle to discovering this policy adaptively is that the leader confirmation round-trip occurs between cluster members, so the client sees only a blended latency signal rather than the decisive coordination cost directly.

</details>


### [112] [Client-side transparent caching for remote ROOT data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00400)
*Dmytro Kovalskyi,Jan Eysermans,Mariarosaria D'Alfonso,Christoph Paus*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: uCache (xrd-ucache) 是一个透明的客户端侧缓存插件，作为 XRootD 客户端插件实现，无需服务器端部署或修改分析代码。它利用分析机器的本地存储作为缓存层，仅存储分析实际读取的数据，并能将数据重建为分支对齐、重新压缩的形式。在 CMS Open Data 基准测试中，填充缓存几乎无额外开销，后续处理速度提升 1.6-15.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理分析过程中，物理学家反复改进算法和测试新想法时，需要重复处理相同数据。随着数据越来越多地从远程存储读取，每次迭代都受网络延迟和带宽影响，共享存储吞吐量在负载下波动显著，导致分析效率低下。

Method: 将 uCache 实现为 XRootD 客户端插件，在分析机器的本地存储与内存之间作为缓存层。缓存只存储分析实际读取的数据，并能将缓存数据重建为分支对齐、重新压缩的形式，以消除后续过程中的大部分输入/输出和减压成本。使用 Analysis Grand Challenge 顶夸克对分析和公开 CMS Open Data（zlib 和 LZMA 压缩）进行基准测试。

Result: 填充缓存相比直接读取几乎无额外开销；后续处理从字节缓存中加速 1.6-8.8 倍，从重新压缩的缓存中加速 2.1-15.7 倍；典型分析中 1 TB 缓存足以支持 10-20 TB 的数据集；当远程数据源负载较重或地理位置较远时，提升最为显著。

Conclusion: uCache 提供了一种高效、透明的客户端侧缓存解决方案，显著降低高能物理分析中重复读取远程数据的开销，在带宽受限或远程存储负载较高的场景下尤为有效。

Abstract: High-energy physics analyses often process the same data as physicists refine algorithms and test new ideas. With data increasingly read from remote storage, each iteration is subject to network latency and depends on network bandwidth and shared-storage throughput, which can vary substantially under load. We present uCache (xrd-ucache), a transparent client-side cache implemented as an XRootD client plugin that requires neither server-side deployment nor changes to analysis code. It uses local storage on the analysis machine as a cache layer between the network and memory. The cache stores only the data actually read by an analysis. It can also rebuild cached data into a branch-aligned, recompressed form, eliminating most of the input/output and decompression costs of subsequent passes. We benchmark the cache using the Analysis Grand Challenge top quark pair analysis on public CMS Open Data compressed with zlib and LZMA. Filling the cache adds essentially no overhead compared with a direct read. Subsequent passes are 1.6-8.8 times faster from the byte cache and 2.1-15.7 times faster from the recompressed cache. For a typical analysis, a 1 TB cache suffices for datasets of 10-20 TB. The largest improvements occur when the remote data source is heavily loaded or geographically distant.

</details>


### [113] [DRLM: Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based LLM Query Orchestration in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00442)
*Reza Farahani,Zoha Azimi Ourimi,Mario Colosi,Lauri Loven,Christian Timmerer,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DRLM，一种基于深度强化学习的边缘环境LLM查询编排框架，通过集成类别条件质量估计器和特征驱动延迟预测器，结合因子化PPO代理实现状态感知的编排决策。在64节点边缘集群上的实验表明，相比基线方法可降低最多51%的推理延迟和67%的排队延迟，且精度损失不超过8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务面临异构查询和多变资源需求的编排挑战，边缘部署虽然降低响应时间，但设备异构性以及模型家族、参数规模和量化级别的多样性使得高效的LLM查询编排变得困难，现有方法难以在延迟、准确率和资源效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: DRLM框架包含两个轻量级预测器：一是类别条件质量估计器，将查询映射到语义类别并推断模型性能；二是特征驱动的延迟预测器，跨模型-设备配置估算推理时间。这些预测与系统状态结合后，输入因子化PPO代理进行状态感知的编排决策。作者还构建了包含223,835条测量的基准数据集（覆盖1258个查询、6个查询类别、8个模型家族、5种量化级别及异构边缘设备）。

Result: 在64节点边缘集群上的评估显示，与三个基线和两种先进方法相比，DRLM可降低最多51%的推理延迟和67%的排队延迟，同时精度损失不超过8%；在高负载场景下延迟改善高达61.4%，展示了鲁棒且稳定的编排性能。

Conclusion: DRLM作为数据驱动的LLM查询编排框架，有效应对了边缘环境中的异构性和动态性挑战，其状态感知的强化学习编排策略在延迟优化与精度权衡方面优于现有方法，为边缘LLM服务的高效调度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services increasingly process heterogeneous queries with diverse latency, accuracy, and resource requirements. While edge deployment reduces response time, the heterogeneity of devices and the diversity of model families, parameter scales, and quantization levels make efficient LLM query orchestration challenging. This paper introduces DRLM, a Deep Reinforcement Learning-based LLM query orchestration framework in edge environments. DRLM integrates two lightweight predictors: (i) a class-conditioned quality estimator that maps queries to semantic categories and infers model performance, and (ii) a feature-driven latency predictor that estimates inference time across model-device configurations. These predictions, combined with system state, feed a factorized Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent that performs state-aware orchestration decisions. To enable data-driven orchestration, we construct a large-scale benchmarking dataset with 223 835 measurements spanning 1258 queries, 6 query classes, 8 model families (32 deployed instances), 5 quantization levels, and heterogeneous edge devices. Evaluation on a 64-node edge cluster and comparison with three baselines and two state-of-the-art methods show that DRLM reduces inference latency by up to 51% and queuing delay by up to 67 %, while incurring at most 8% accuracy loss. It improves latency under increasing workloads up to 61.4%, demonstrating robust and stable orchestration.

</details>


### [114] [Comparison of Algebraic Block Multi-Coloring and Leiden Methods for Parallel Preconditioning in the ICCG Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00561)
*Tomohiro Suzuki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估将Leiden社区检测方法作为ICCG方法中并行预条件处理的替代分块方案。与需要预先指定块数的ABMC方法不同，Leiden方法能自动生成反映矩阵结构的分块。实验表明，采用constant Potts模型的Leiden方法在性能上可与优化配置的ABMC方法相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ICCG方法的并行化中，不完全Cholesky预条件处理的前向/后向替换存在顺序依赖，成为多核环境下并行化的主要瓶颈。ABMC方法虽能解决该问题，但需要预先指定块数作为输入参数，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 将Leiden社区检测方法引入并行预条件处理，将其作为稀疏矩阵邻接图的自动分块工具。使用模块度和constant Potts模型作为质量函数实现Leiden方法，并与ABMC方法在迭代次数、执行时间和L2缓存效率等方面进行比较，实验涵盖八个对称正定矩阵。

Result: 实验结果表明，采用constant Potts模型的Leiden方法在并行ICCG预条件处理中取得了与优化块数配置下的ABMC方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: Leiden方法作为自动分块机制，免除了ABMC方法需预设块数的限制，同时保持接近最优ABMC配置的性能，是一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: In the application of incomplete Cholesky preconditioning to the incomplete Cholesky-conjugate gradient (ICCG) method, forward and backward substitutions exhibit sequential dependencies that constitute a major bottleneck for parallelization in multicore environments. To alleviate this bottleneck, the algebraic block multi-coloring (ABMC) method achieves both parallelism and data locality through block-wise coloring. However, ABMC requires the number of blocks to be specified as an input parameter in advance. This study evaluates the Leiden method as an alternative blocking approach for parallel preconditioning in the ICCG method. As a community detection technique that maximizes a quality function for graph partitioning, the Leiden method automatically generates blocks that reflect the matrix structure without requiring the number of blocks a priori. We partition the adjacency graphs of sparse matrices using the Leiden method and utilize the resulting blocks for parallel preconditioning. We implement the Leiden method using modularity and the constant Potts model as quality functions and compare its performance with that of the ABMC method in terms of the number of iterations, execution time, and L2 cache efficiency across eight symmetric positive definite matrices. The experimental results demonstrate that the Leiden method with the constant Potts model achieves performance comparable to that of the ABMC method configured with an optimized number of blocks.

</details>


### [115] [Characterizing the Scalability and Performance of Large-Scale AI Training Under Multi-Tenancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00817)
*Jacopo Raffi,Thomas Pasquali,Lorenzo Piarulli,Filippo Spiga,Marco Faltelli,Andreas Herten,Domenico Siracusa,Daniele De Sensi,Flavio Vella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究在多达2400个GPU的现代HPC系统上，系统性地评估了AI模型在不同互连技术、并行化策略和多租户并发场景下的性能与可扩展性。作者设计了包含多种并行化策略的基准测试套件，在Alps、Leonardo、LUMI、JUPITER、NVL72 GB300和DGX A100等多个超算集群上量化了通信开销，并提出了一个现实噪声模型来研究并发训练任务之间的干扰效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和刻画AI工作负载在高性能计算系统上的性能表现，既需要了解其在隔离环境下的可扩展性，也需要掌握其在并发执行时的行为。然而，并行化策略、网络拥塞、计算能力和互连技术之间的相互作用仍然研究不足，这阻碍了对分布式AI训练性能的系统性理解。

Method: 本文设计了一套AI模型基准测试套件，在六个不同的超算集群上评估五种并行化策略（包括scale-up、scale-out和rack-scale配置）在不同资源分配方案下的表现，并设计了一个现实噪声模型来模拟和分析多个并发训练作业之间的相互干扰。

Result: 在多达2400个GPU的规模下，量化了通信开销及其对不同互连技术的性能影响，提供了跨多个超算平台的系统性能特征描述，揭示了分布式AI训练在可扩展性和执行效率方面的关键性能模式，并针对多租户场景下的并发干扰行为提供了重要见解。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI分布式训练在HPC系统上的可扩展性和执行效率提供了系统性的刻画框架，为理解现代HPC环境下多租户竞争场景中的真实性能行为提供了关键洞见，有助于指导未来AI工作负载在超大规模计算基础设施上的部署与优化。

Abstract: Characterising AI workload performance on modern HPC systems requires understanding both their scalability in isolation and their behaviour under concurrent execution. However, the interplay among parallelisation strategies, network congestion, compute capability, and interconnect technologies remains poorly understood. This work investigates the performance and scalability of AI models up to 2400 GPUs. We quantify the communication overheads and their impact across different interconnects by evaluating scale-up, scale-out, and rack-scale configurations under multiple allocation schemes. Finally, we study how multiple concurrent training jobs interfere with each other by designing a realistic noise model. We design a benchmark suite of AI models to evaluate the performance of five distinct parallelisation strategies across different supercomputing clusters, including Alps, Leonardo, LUMI, JUPITER, NVL72 GB300, and DGX A100. Our work provides a systematic characterization of the scalability and execution efficiency of distributed AI training, while offering key insights into performance behavior under realistic multi-tenant scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [Breaking Cycles for Scalable Fair Ordering in Blockchain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00837)
*Jinchun He,Wangjie Qiu,Yizhong Liu,Shengda Zhuo,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashOrder，一种确定性公平排序引擎，通过将循环模糊性局部化（而非批级SCC凝聚）来处理区块链交易排序中的Condorcet循环问题。它以一维规范位置嵌入成对偏好，利用划分超图聚类邻近交易，并执行分层簇内外序列化，在公平性和吞吐量上均大幅优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链中交易顺序直接影响金融结果，不公平排序导致前端攻击和夹击攻击，已从以太坊用户窃取超6.86亿美元。现有公平排序协议在竞争或恶意操纵下遭遇Condorcet循环，被迫进行全局强连通分量（SCC）凝聚，导致延迟增加、批次粗糙和扩展性失败。

Method: FlashOrder将成对偏好嵌入一维规范位置，通过划分超图对邻近交易聚类，并执行层级的簇间与簇内序列化，以局部化的排序和聚合取代批级SCC凝聚，从而在循环模糊性跨批次传播前将其定位。

Result: 在基于libhotstuff的原型上对照Themis（CCS '23）和Rashnu（VLDB '24）评估，FlashOrder吞吐量比Themis高10.5倍、比Rashnu高4.8倍，且延迟差距随网络规模扩大而增大。在受控对抗模拟中，最大排名位移减少88.7%；在Condorcet攻击下，平均吞吐量比Themis和Rashnu分别高12.0倍和9.7倍。

Conclusion: 局部化循环模糊性可以在显著更高吞吐量的前提下实现更强的公平性，验证了FlashOrder在应对区块链排序中恶意操纵和高竞争场景时的有效性。

Abstract: In blockchain systems, transaction order directly determines financial outcomes: unfair ordering enables front-running and sandwich attacks that have extracted over \$686M from Ethereum users. Current fair-ordering protocols aggregate pairwise receive-order evidence from replicas. Under contention or adversarial manipulation, however, Condorcet cycles force them into global strongly connected component (SCC) condensation, causing delays, coarse batches, and scaling failures.
  We present FlashOrder, a deterministic fair-ordering engine that localizes cyclic ambiguity before it propagates across the batch. FlashOrder embeds pairwise preferences into one-dimensional canonical positions, clusters nearby transactions with a partition hypergraph, and performs hierarchical inter- and intra-cluster serialization, replacing batch-wide SCC condensation with localized sorting and aggregation. Evaluated against Themis (CCS '23) and Rashnu (VLDB '24) on a libhotstuff-based prototype, FlashOrder achieves up to 10.5$\times$ higher throughput than Themis and 4.8$\times$ higher than Rashnu, with the latency gap widening as network scales. In controlled adversarial simulation, it reduces maximum rank displacement by 88.7\%, and under Condorcet attacks it sustains 12.0$\times$ and 9.7$\times$ higher throughput than Themis and Rashnu on average. These results show that localizing cyclic ambiguity yields stronger fairness at substantially higher throughput.

</details>


### [117] [Shared-Memory Range-Tiled CDF Sort for Small-Range Integer Keys on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00843)
*Kento Ando,Kaito Takase,Noriyuki Fujimoto,Koichi Wada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study unstable integer sorting on GPUs for arrays whose elements lie in a known integer range. Focusing on counting-sort-based methods that determine the output interval of each value from its frequency and the prefix sums of the frequencies, we propose and evaluate Range-Tiled CDF sort (RT-CDF), which partitions the possible value range into small intervals, called tiles, that fit in shared memory. For each tile, RT-CDF constructs a histogram, computes its prefix sum as a local CDF, and directly generates the output array from the local CDF. We compare RT-CDF against three baselines: CUB DeviceRadixSort, whose processed bit range is restricted to $[0,\lceil\log_2 R\rceil)$ to exploit the known range size $R$; Ref-H-P sort; and an implementation based on the algorithm of Kolonias et al. Experiments on an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 with range sizes from $R=2^7$ to $2^{18}$, input sizes from $n=10^6$ to $10^9$, and uniformly distributed, normally distributed, and all-equal inputs show that RT-CDF outperforms the baselines over a broad set of conditions for small to medium ranges, achieving a maximum speedup of 4.39 over the fastest baseline. For $R=2^{18}$, however, at least one baseline outperforms RT-CDF for every evaluated input size and input distribution, showing that the cost of histogram construction limits the applicability of RT-CDF to larger ranges.

</details>


### [118] [Prediction-Robust Service Deployment with Capacity-Aware Edge Admission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00877)
*Hailiang Zhao,Ziqi Wang,Yifei Zhang,Mingyi Liu,Xinkui Zhao,Kingsum Chow,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPSUM及其弹性变体CAPSUM-E，用于边缘平台中带有TTL的在线服务部署决策，通过容量感知的准入策略结合影子价格和证据密度驱逐机制，在预测敏感和容量耦合的约束下显著降低部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台在用户附近实例化可执行服务以减少请求服务成本，但每个实例需要一次性部署成本且只能在有限TTL内有效。现有的在线决策既对预测敏感又受容量耦合影响：乐观预测浪费部署成本，延迟决策则错过服务突发。

Method: 提出CAPSUM容量感知准入策略及弹性特化CAPSUM-E。核心方法包括：局部弹性设置下将节点-服务轨迹归约为变价Bahncard实例以继承PFSUM的紧致预测误差依赖比率；容量有限节点上结合尺寸缩放盈亏平衡测试、利用率依赖影子价格和证据密度驱逐机制；实现精确的局部离线动态规划。

Result: CAPSUM在共同模型下相对最佳源衍生适配器（EDP-A、OREO、uEDC-L）在合成场景中降低标准化成本33.7-42.9%，在采样轨迹上降低45.5%。实验覆盖受控预测误差、三种合成需求模式、Globus Compute公共轨迹上的因果预测器，以及扩展至1024节点和10000服务的联合规模测试。

Conclusion: CAPSUM通过弹性配置与CAPSUM-E精确一致，验证了容量可行性、尺度不变性，并展示了在预测误差和容量耦合约束下对现有方法的显著成本优势。

Abstract: Edge platforms instantiate executable services close to users to reduce request-serving cost, but each instance incurs a one-time deployment cost and remains useful only for a finite time-to-live (TTL). The resulting online decision is both prediction-sensitive and capacity-coupled: an optimistic forecast can waste deployment cost, whereas a delayed decision misses the burst it is intended to serve. We study this problem under a common TTL cost model and propose CAPSUM, a capacity-aware admission policy with an elastic specialization, CAPSUM-E. In the local elastic setting, every node-service trace is exactly a variable-price Bahncard instance. This reduction lets CAPSUM-E inherit PFSUM's tight prediction-error-dependent ratio, including $2/(1+β)$ consistency and $1/β$ robustness for $β>0$. A redirect-aware variant preserves the same local deployment schedule. For finite-capacity nodes, CAPSUM combines size-scaled break-even tests, a utilization-dependent shadow price, and evidence-density eviction; we prove capacity feasibility, scale invariance, and exact agreement with CAPSUM-E under an elastic configuration. We implement an exact local offline dynamic program and compare against direct common-model baselines and documented source-derived adapters for EDP-A, OREO, and uEDC-L. Experiments cover controlled prediction error, three synthetic demand regimes, a causal predictor on a public Globus Compute trace, and joint scaling to 1,024 nodes and 10,000 services. Under the common model, CAPSUM reduces normalized cost by 33.7-42.9% relative to the best source-derived adapter across the synthetic regimes and by 45.5% on the sampled trace.

</details>


### [119] [AInfer-PD: Communication-Safe In-Place Prefill-Decode Multiplexing for Distributed MoE Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00993)
*Guowei Wang,Chaokun Yang,Zhenxuan Pan,Yuhong Guo,Minghua Zhu,Zhechuan Zhang,Shuo Wan,Xiaowei Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AInfer-PD，一种面向分布式MoE rollout场景的in-place预填充（P）/解码（D）多路复用方案，通过协调跨rank的P/D集合通信顺序并为DeepEP的两个通信路径提供独立状态，解决P/D并发执行时通信冲突问题，在保持共享权重和KV存储的同时将fixed-workload rollout完成时间降低18-35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模强化学习中，rollout推理常占据墙钟时间的主导。在agentic RL中，异步轨迹使得预填充（P）和解码（D）的共存成为rollout的持续性特征而非一次性事件。在共享加速器上，P/D共存会导致prefill干扰延迟敏感的decode，而P/D分离需要额外设备池和KV缓存传输，现有的in-place多路复用设计缺乏MoE大规模部署所需的通信隔离能力。

Method: AInfer-PD扩展了in-place P/D多路复用到分布式MoE rollout场景。具体包括：(1) 跨rank协调P/D的集合通信顺序；(2) 为DeepEP的两条路径（P和D）提供独立的通信状态，使交叉的ADP/ATP和DeepEP路径在并发P/D执行下保持安全。该设计在共享模型权重和KV存储的同时，在同一设备上协调P和D。

Result: 在单节点repeated prefill-intensive workloads上，AInfer-PD相比禁用P/D多路复用的同一AInfer引擎将fixed-workload rollout完成时间降低7.1-22.5%，相比SGLang降低24.8-32.9%。在两节点场景下降低分别为18.0-35.3%和18.3-31.8%。同引擎消融实验中，细粒度边界相比whole-epoch异步入队进一步降低8.6-19.8%的完成时间。

Conclusion: AInfer-PD通过巧妙的通信协调设计，使得在共享设备上并发执行P和D成为可能且效率显著提升，避免了P/D分离所需的额外资源开销，为大规模MoE agentic RL的rollout推理提供了一个高效的加速方案。

Abstract: Rollout inference often dominates the wall-clock time of large-scale reinforcement learning (RL). In agentic RL, each trajectory alternates between model generation and environment interaction over multiple turns. Asynchronous trajectories consequently introduce new prefill (P) work while other trajectories remain in decode (D), making P/D coexistence a persistent property of the rollout rather than a one-time prompt-ingestion event.
  On shared accelerators, persistent P/D coexistence can make prefill interfere with latency-sensitive decode and prolong rollout completion. P/D disaggregation avoids this co-location but requires separate device pools and KV-cache transfers. In-place multiplexing retains shared devices and KV state, but existing designs lack the communication isolation needed for large MoE deployments that combine attention TP/DP with distributed expert execution. In practical implementations, P and D can issue intersecting collectives in inconsistent cross-rank orders; DeepEP's P and D paths also share mutable protocol state.
  We present AInfer-PD, which extends in-place P/D multiplexing to distributed MoE rollouts. AInfer-PD coordinates P/D collective order across ranks and gives the two DeepEP paths independent communication state, making crossed ADP/ATP and DeepEP paths safe for concurrent P/D execution. The design retains shared model weights and KV storage while coordinating P and D on the same devices. Across repeated single-node prefill-intensive workloads, AInfer-PD reduces fixed-workload rollout completion time by 7.1-22.5% relative to the same AInfer engine with P/D multiplexing disabled and by 24.8-32.9% relative to SGLang. On two nodes, the reductions are 18.0-35.3% and 18.3-31.8%, respectively. In a same-engine ablation, fine-grained boundaries reduce completion time by a further 8.6-19.8% over whole-epoch asynchronous enqueue.

</details>


### [120] [MakoXC: Rearchitecting DFT Exchange-Correlation with Matrix-Aligned and Knowledge-Organized Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01025)
*Haozhi Han,Fusong Ju,Jing Bai,Ruge Zhang,Xiang Zhao,Liang Yuan,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Liu Yunxin,Yifeng Chen,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MakoXC是一种模块化矩阵对齐的交换相关（XC）评估引擎，通过将近程性诱导的稀疏性重构为规则、加速器友好的计算，平均比标准XC评估快67.8倍，比最先进的线性缩放方法快4.7倍，并能在64个GPU上将泛素（1,231个原子）的端到端DFT计算缩短至5分钟以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFT中的交换相关（XC）评估由于三次方缩放复杂度成为主要瓶颈，尽管线性缩放方法利用电子近程性降低了渐近复杂度，但其产生的不规则稀疏工作负载隐藏了隐式稀疏性，无法高效利用现代AI加速器。

Method: MakoXC协同设计了三种关键技术：(1) 矩阵对齐单元（Matrix-Aligned Cells），将近程性诱导的相互作用重组为密集、加速器对齐的数据簇；(2) 稀疏引导激活（Sparsity-Guided Activation），将更深层的隐式稀疏性转化为数值正确的结构化执行；(3) 核融合流水线（Kernel-Fused Pipeline），将碎片化工作负载整合为统一的计算密集型执行路径。

Result: 平均加速比为标准XC评估的67.8倍、最先进线性缩放方法的4.7倍；集成到商业级DFT软件包后，可在64个GPU上将泛素（1,231个原子，def2-SVP基组）的端到端DFT计算在5分钟内完成。

Conclusion: MakoXC通过将XC评估重构为统一的结构化计算，展示了科学工作负载如何在AI加速器上实现真正的低复杂度和最大并行效率，弥合了算法复杂度与硬件利用率之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Density Functional Theory (DFT) is indispensable for materials science and drug discovery, yet the exchange--correlation (XC) evaluation remains a major bottleneck due to its cubic scaling. Although linear-scaling methods exploit electronic nearsightedness to reduce asymptotic complexity, they produce irregular sparse workloads that hide implicit sparsity and prevent efficient use of modern AI accelerators. We present MakoXC, a modular matrix-aligned XC evaluation engine that rearchitects nearsightedness-induced sparsity into regular, accelerator-friendly computations. MakoXC co-designs three key techniques: (1) Matrix-Aligned Cells reorganize nearsightedness-induced interactions into dense, accelerator-aligned data clusters; (2) Sparsity-Guided Activation translates deeper implicit sparsity into numerically correct structured execution for practical linear scaling; and (3) Kernel-Fused Pipeline consolidates fragmented workloads into a unified, compute-intensive execution path that fully unleashes accelerator throughput. Extensive evaluations show that MakoXC achieves average speedups of 67.8$\times$ speedup over standard XC evaluation and 4.7$\times$ over state-of-the-art linear-scaling methods. When integrated into a production-grade commercial DFT package, MakoXC scales XC evaluation to ubiquitin (1,231 atoms, def2-SVP) on 64 GPUs, enabling the end-to-end DFT calculation to complete in under five minutes. By restructuring XC evaluation into a unified, structured computation, MakoXC demonstrates how scientific workloads can achieve genuine low complexity while maximizing parallel efficiency on AI accelerators.

</details>


### [121] [Update for Decisions, Not Freshness: Goal-Oriented Status Updating and Selective Offloading at the Network Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01082)
*Jianpeng Qi,Qiyang Zhang,Chao Liu,Jing Sun,Yimei Liu,Yanwei Yu,Yingjie Wang,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CoSMO是一个面向边缘-云协作环境的协同事件驱动强化学习框架，通过联合设计语义状态管理与选择性卸载决策，将状态更新的价值直接关联到任务决策效用上，在按时完成率和容量感知决策精度上相较最优对比方法分别提升18.6%-21.2%和17.6%-17.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘-云协同环境中，边缘节点面临任务本地执行/转发/拒绝的决策，但只能通过间歇性刷新的缓存间接感知服务节点状态。传统基于信息年龄（AoI）等新鲜度驱动的方案没有直接将状态更新的价值与其对后续任务决策的影响联系起来，缺乏对部分可观测性下异步闭环系统的高效协同优化。

Method: 提出CoSMO框架，采用协同事件驱动强化学习：在服务节点端使用循环半马尔可夫双深度Q网络（Double DQN）智能体联合决策是否发送状态及下一决策间隔；在边缘节点端使用基于任务终止的离策略价值学习智能体，基于本地观测和过期的远程语义信息进行分层门控-路由决策。两智能体保持独立观测与价值目标，但共享同一实际任务效用流，无需集中式执行。

Result: 在评估的各种工作负载族中，CoSMO相对于最佳竞争方法的按时完成率平均提升18.6%-21.2%；在三个严格过载场景下，容量感知决策精度的平均增益为17.6%-17.9%。

Conclusion: CoSMO验证了将状态管理（何时发送语义状态）与任务控制（如何卸载/执行任务）在共享任务效用目标下协同设计的有效性，表明在部分可观测的异步边缘计算场景中，通过学习紧凑的异构服务状态表示并采用去中心化的事件驱动强化学习，能够显著超越仅关注信息新鲜度的传统方案。

Abstract: In an edge--cloud collaborative edge-computing environment, an edge node (EN) must decide whether each user task should be executed locally, forwarded to a remote service (or cloud) node (SN), or rejected. The EN observes its local state directly but receives the SN state only through an intermittently refreshed cache. Status updating and task control therefore form an asynchronous closed loop under partial observability. Freshness-driven schemes, including those based on Age of Information (AoI), do not directly value an update by its effect on subsequent task decisions. We propose CoSMO (Co-design of Semantic-state Management and Offloading), a cooperative event-driven reinforcement learning (RL) framework that coordinates semantic status management and selective offloading through realized task utility. CoSMO learns a compact representation of the heterogeneous SN service state. At the SN, a recurrent semi-Markov double deep Q-network (Double DQN) agent jointly selects send/no-send and the next decision interval. At the EN, a task-terminal off-policy value-learning agent makes hierarchical gate--route decisions from local observations and stale remote semantics. The agents maintain separate observations and value targets but share the same realized task-utility stream, without centralized execution. Across the evaluated workload families, CoSMO's reported relative improvement in on-time completion rate over the best-performing competing method averages 18.6%--21.2%. For capacity-aware decision accuracy across the three strict-overload points, the corresponding reported gains average 17.6%--$17.9%.

</details>


### [122] [Just Talk Once: Communication-Efficient Split Federated LLM Fine-Tuning on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01457)
*Jiaxiang Geng,Xianhao Chen,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出L-shaped SFT及其扩展one-shot SFT，利用现代大语言模型中权重共享的特性，使服务器端可通过目标嵌入直接监督隐藏激活值，从而消除传统split federated fine-tuning中服务器与客户端之间的双向通信瓶颈，并允许客户端一次性上传激活值后离线，大幅降低通信成本和客户端在线时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM微调越来越依赖边缘设备生成的数据，但边缘设备面临内存、计算、带宽和连接性限制，传统联邦学习难以持续。现有的Split联邦微调（SFT）虽然将大部分模型参数和计算卸载到服务器，但需要逐步骤的双向通信循环，且要求客户端在整个训练过程中持续参与，这成为效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出L-shaped SFT框架，核心洞察是现代LLM中的权重共享（weight tying）机制使得服务器端隐藏激活值可以直接通过目标嵌入进行监督，从而在服务器端计算训练损失，无需将服务器输出返回给客户端，消除了双向通信瓶颈。进一步基于此提出one-shot SFT，客户端仅需上传一次激活值即可离线，服务器端在缓存的表示上继续优化。

Result: 在包含商用智能手机和NVIDIA开发板等异构边缘客户端的真实系统测试平台上实现验证，实验表明所提方案相比现有SFT基线显著降低了通信成本和客户端在线时间。

Conclusion: L-shaped SFT和one-shot SFT有效解决了split联邦微调中的双向通信瓶颈和客户端持续参与问题，为边缘端LLM联邦微调提供了一种更高效的可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning is increasingly shifting toward data generated on edge devices, where memory, computation, bandwidth, and connectivity constraints make conventional federated learning difficult to sustain. Split federated fine-tuning (SFT) improves client-side efficiency by offloading most model parameters and computation to the server but requires step-by-step bidirectional communication loop across the split interface and forces continuous client involvement throughout training. In this paper, we present L-shaped SFT, a split fine-tuning framework that removes this bidirectional bottleneck. Our key insight is that weight tying in modern LLMs enables server-side hidden activations to be directly supervised using target embeddings, allowing the training loss to be computed on the server without returning server outputs to the client. To further eliminate the need for continuous client participation, based on L-shaped SFT, we introduce one-shot SFT, in which clients upload activations once and then go offline while the server continues optimization over cached representations. We implement our design in a real system testbed with heterogeneous edge clients, including commercial smartphones and NVIDIA developer boards. Experiments demonstrate that our schemes significantly reduce communication costs and client online time compared with existing SFT baselines.

</details>


### [123] [Faster Convergence of Multidimensional Approximate Agreement via Smallest Enclosing Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01490)
*Darya Melnyk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出BallMidpoint协议，一种新的多维近似一致协议，其收缩率为1/√2≈0.707，在同步和异步通信模型下均适用，显著优于此前最优的√(7/8)≈0.935，且具有最优容错性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多维近似一致协议的收缩率虽小于1足以保证收敛，但远高于一维情形下最优下界1/2，不利于实际应用。作者希望缩小收缩率与理论下界之间的差距。

Method: 提出BallMidpoint算法，核心思想是选取所有局部安全区域的最小包围球的中心作为下一轮的输出点。该算法无需坐标参考，满足凸有效性下的最优弹性，且点可在安全区域内高效计算。

Result: 在同步和异步通信模型下，BallMidpoint协议实现1/√2≈0.707的收缩率，远优于此前最优的0.935，并且该收缩率对所述算法是紧的。这是首个收缩率更接近已知下界而非上界1的多维近似一致协议。

Conclusion: BallMidpoint将多维近似一致的收缩率从0.935大幅提升至0.707，显著拉近与一维最优下界1/2的距离，是该领域的重要突破。

Abstract: This work considers the multidimensional approximate agreement problem. In this problem, $n$ parties in a distributed system, up to $t$ of which may be corrupted by a Byzantine adversary, need to output vectors that are close to each other and that lie inside the convex hull of all non-corrupted input vectors. We assume that nodes communicate in a fully-connected authenticated network and analyze synchronous and asynchronous communication models. The focus of this work is on the contraction factor of approximate agreement protocols.
  The first multidimensional approximate agreement protocols had a contraction rate of $1-1/n$(VG, PODC'13) and $\sqrt[d]{1/2}$(MH, STOC'13). While a rate below $1$ is sufficient for convergence, it is not sufficient for practical applications. To date, the best known convergence rate of approximate agreement algorithms is $\sqrt{7/8}\approx0.935$ (FN, DISC'18), which is achieved through the MidExtremes protocol. This stands in contrast to the lower bound on the convergence rate in the $1$-dimensional setting, which is $1/2$.
  In this work, we propose BallMidpoint - a novel approximate agreement protocol with a contraction rate of $1/\sqrt{2}\approx 0.707$ in the synchronous and the asynchronous communication models. This algorithm satisfies the optimal resilience under convex validity. The presented contraction rate is achieved by choosing the midpoint of the smallest enclosing ball of the so-called local safe areas, and it is tight for the presented algorithms. Similar to (FN, DISC'18), our algorithm is coordinate-free, and the point inside the safe area can be computed efficiently. This work presents the first multidimensional approximate agreement protocol where the convergence rate is closer to the best known lower bound rather than the upper bound of $1$.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Can LLMs Design Video Coding Tools? A Case Study on Planar Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01535)
*Yingwen Zhang,Meng Wang,Liqiang He,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文探索了利用大型语言模型（LLM）设计视频编码工具的可能性，以Planar模式这一视频编码标准中的长期存在的帧内预测工具为案例，通过"生成-评估-再生成"的闭环流程，验证了LLM生成的预测器在VVenC和ECM编码器上均能带来编码增益，初步证明了LLM辅助视频编码工具设计的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编码工具的设计涉及工具修改之间复杂的算法耦合，极具挑战性。传统方法依赖领域专家的手工设计和调优，作者希望探索LLM能否自动完成这一高度专业化的任务，以降低人工设计成本并可能发现超越人类经验的编码工具。

Method: 采用生成-评估闭环框架：LLM生成新的Planar预测器实现，编码器实际运行并评估编码性能，将评估结果作为反馈输入给LLM促使其生成改进版本。在VVenC（快速预设）下直接替换默认Planar模式；在ECM中基于新增的方向性Planar模式，探索了直接替换和添加为带新语法元素的额外预测模式两种集成策略。

Result: 在VVenC快速预设下，LLM生成的Planar模式相比传统模式在标准基准上实现了0.18%的码率节省，仅增加0.4%的计算复杂度开销。在ECM的受限低分辨率设定下，两种集成策略均能带来编码增益。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM驱动视频编码工具设计的初步证据和实践经验，表明LLM在该领域具有应用潜力，同时也揭示了这一方向仍面临诸多开放性挑战。研究结果在受约束的轻量级工具集和低分辨率设置下验证了可行性，但距离大规模实际应用仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: This paper explores whether large language models (LLMs) can design video coding tools, a highly challenging task due to the intricate algorithmic coupling of tool modifications. In particular, we present an empirical case study on the Planar mode, a long-standing intra prediction tool in video coding standards. Our experiments operate within a generation-and-evaluation loop, with the LLM generating new Planar predictors, encoder trials evaluating their coding performance, and the LLM re-generating refined implementations based on the evaluation feedback. We first examine directly replacing the default Planar mode in the Fraunhofer Versatile Video Encoder (VVenC) under its faster preset. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM-generated mode can outperform the conventional Planar mode on this lightweight toolset, achieving 0.18% bitrate savings with 0.4% complexity overhead on the standard benchmark. We further extend our evaluation to the Enhanced Compression Model (ECM). Leveraging newly introduced directional Planar modes, we investigate two integration strategies: directly replacing them, and introducing the LLM-generated predictor as an additional prediction mode with new syntax elements. The empirical results suggest that both strategies can yield coding gains under a constrained low-resolution setting. Overall, this study offers preliminary evidence and practical insights, highlighting both the potential and open challenges of LLM-based coding tool design.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Intelligent Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00181)
*Kalgi Gandhi,Minal Bhise*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出WACI-HJ（负载感知列印记哈希连接），一种面向大规模边缘系统的负载感知哈希连接加速方法。它通过预测到来查询的工作负载，提前完成分箱和哈希等预处理，从而减少不必要的cacheline扫描。在基准数据集和智能交通真实数据集上，WACI-HJ相比现有最优方法CI-HJ实现了54%的cacheline读取减少和10%的查询执行时间提升，并在CPU、RAM、I/O方面分别获得1%、38%、49%的增益，适用于实时边缘查询处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备数量快速增长，但其处理能力、内存和网络带宽有限，使得边缘查询处理中的资源利用和数据管理面临挑战。连接（Join）是数据库中最耗时的操作之一。现有最优方法CI-HJ虽通过等高分箱加速哈希连接，但在实时处理上效率不足，且会扫描不必要的cacheline，因此需要一种更高效、适合实时边缘环境的连接加速方案。

Method: WACI-HJ采用负载感知方法加速哈希连接，包含两个阶段：（1）生成阶段，由预处理、预测、分块与哈希三个模块组成，在查询到达前基于预测的工作负载预先计算分箱；（2）查询处理与资源利用阶段，负责查询执行以及对CPU、RAM和I/O资源的监控与利用。通过预先预测即将到来的查询工作负载，进一步增强其适用于实时边缘查询处理的能力。

Result: 在基准数据集和智能交通真实数据集上的实验表明，WACI-HJ相比CI-HJ实现了54%的cacheline读取减少和10%的查询执行时间提升。该技术对缩放数据和偏斜数据均有效。能量效率实验显示，WACI-HJ在CPU、RAM和I/O方面分别获得1%、38%和49%的增益。

Conclusion: WACI-HJ通过优化缓存使用和查询执行速度，加速了智能交通场景中的实时交通分析、拥堵管理和路线规划。该技术还可推广到其他领域以加速边缘查询处理，展示了良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: The number of edge devices in large-scale edge systems is rapidly increasing. Edge devices have limited processing power, memory, and network bandwidth, making resource utilization and data management during edge query processing challenging. Joins are among the costliest database operations in terms of time and resources. The State-of-the-Art edge query processing, Column Imprint-Hash Join CI-HJ, addresses this challenge using equi-height binning to accelerate hash joins. However, it lacks efficiency in real-time processing and scans unnecessary cachelines. This paper presents Workload Aware Column Imprint-Hash Join WACI-HJ, which uses a workload-aware approach to accelerate hash joins. Predicting the upcoming query workload in advance further improves its suitability for real-time edge query processing. WACI-HJ comprises two phases: WACI-HJ Generation Phase, including Pre-processing, Prediction, and Blocking and Hashing modules to compute bins based on the predicted workload before query arrival, and Query Processing and Resource Utilization, which handles query processing and CPU, RAM, and I/O utilization. Evaluations on a benchmark dataset and a real-world Smart Transportation dataset show a 54% reduction in cachelines read and 10% improved query execution time. The proposed technique is effective for both scaled and skewed data. Although PCR is an indirect measure of energy consumption, the work also directly measures energy consumption through energy-efficiency experiments. WACI-HJ shows 1%, 38%, and 49% gain in CPU, RAM, and I/O, respectively. Optimizing cache usage and query execution speeds up real-time traffic analysis, congestion management, and routing in Smart Transportation. Additionally, this technology can be applied to other domains to accelerate edge query processing.

</details>


### [126] [Bounded, Indeterminate, or a Bug: A Condition-Aware Oracle for Differential Testing of SQL Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00381)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对浮点聚合函数的差分数据库测试不sound的问题，提出了以精确有理数为基准的新oracle，将每个差异分类为精确、有界或不确定，并给出了可分性边界κ*。实验表明可恢复算法指数（如ClickHouse为单遍算法p=2.05），且该引擎在方差上存在不可测试的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分数据库测试在浮点聚合上不sound——不同引擎因浮点运算不可结合而合法地产生分歧。实践用epsilon补丁或干脆避开浮点。需要一种能正确区分bug与舍入误差的oracle。

Method: 以存储的double精确有理值为ground truth（而非另一个引擎），每种差异被分类为exact、bounded或indeterminate；通过相对误差界rel_err <= C_A(n,u)*κ_f^p推导可测试性边界κ*=(1/C_A)^{1/p}；SUM/AVG为p=1线性情形，方差单遍算法p=2、Welford为p=1。

Result: 在8个引擎四类中测量指数可恢复各算法；ClickHouse是唯一单遍引擎（p=2.05），引擎级返回零标准差、NaN相关和错误符号回归，而其他引擎保持精确；其方差在条件数比SUM低10^6处已不可测试，普通存储约定（epoch纳秒时间戳、紧密传感器）即会越过该边界，ClickHouse此处误差2100%；360次随机测试零异常。

Conclusion: 该oracle为差分测试提供了浮点聚合场景下缺失的sound判定基础，其决定性因素不是查询本身而是引擎的算法；同时揭示了ClickHouse方差实现的单遍算法缺陷并得到供应商Welford修复确认，证明oracle有效且可靠。

Abstract: Differential database testing compares results across engines and calls a discrepancy a bug. For floating-point aggregates this is unsound: engines legitimately disagree because floating-point arithmetic is not associative. Practice patches this with an epsilon; the leading oracles avoid floating point entirely. We give the oracle this practice lacks, and show its decisive quantity is not the query but the engine's algorithm. Ground truth is the exact rational value of the stored doubles -- arithmetic, not another engine -- and each discrepancy is classified exact, bounded, or indeterminate. The relative error of an aggregate f under an algorithm A obeys rel_err <= C_A(n,u) * kappa_f^p, so the testability boundary, beyond which no oracle can separate a bug from rounding, is kappa*_{f,A} = (1/C_A)^{1/p}. SUM and AVG are the linear case p=1; variance is p=2 for the one-pass algorithm and p=1 for Welford. Across eight engines in four classes the measured exponent recovers each algorithm, and ClickHouse is the lone one-pass engine (p=2.05); engine-wide, it returns zero standard deviation, NaN correlation and wrong-sign regression, while every other engine stays exact and the vendor ships the Welford fix. Its variance is untestable at a condition number 10^6 below SUM's, which ordinary storage conventions (epoch-nanosecond timestamps, tight sensors) cross -- there ClickHouse errs by 2100%. A randomised hunt of 360 tests finds zero anomalies, evidence the oracle is sound. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [127] [Time-Decayed Vector Search in the Rhythm of TANGO: Jointly Modeling Semantic Similarity and Temporal Freshness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00548)
*Jiuqi Wei,Qiyao Luo,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间衰减向量搜索（TDVS）问题，提出STR方法使现有MIPS索引支持TDVS，并设计了Chronos框架和TANGO层次图索引，在查询吞吐量和索引构建速度上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统向量搜索仅基于固定评分函数衡量语义相似度，但在许多应用中相关性会随时间演变，需要将时间新鲜度纳入搜索目标，现有方法无法有效支持这种时间衰减的搜索需求。

Method: 首先形式化时间衰减向量搜索（TDVS）问题；提出Score-Preserving Temporal Reduction (STR)使现有最大内积搜索索引支持TDVS；设计Chronos框架，引入Query-Orthogonal TimeLift精确保持查询-数据分数和排名同时控制数据几何；基于Chronos提出TANGO层次图索引，在不同层级采用特定TimeLift几何，并结合精确TDVS分数遍历、时间因子缓存和在线插入支持。

Result: TANGO相比最先进的基于图的竞争者实现了最高3.5倍查询吞吐量提升和4.05倍索引构建加速，并在多种时间设置下保持优势，同时支持高效的在线插入。

Conclusion: TANGO通过层次化的TimeLift几何设计，在保持语义连通性的同时有效融入时间新鲜度信号，展示了在时间衰减向量搜索任务上的鲁棒性和实用性，为时序敏感的向量检索提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Vector search typically measures relevance through semantic similarity under a fixed scoring function. However, in a growing range of applications, relevance may evolve over time, making temporal freshness an additional signal beyond semantic similarity. In this paper, we formalize time-decayed vector search (TDVS), which incorporates continuous temporal decay into the search objective so that relevance is jointly determined by semantic similarity and temporal freshness. We design Score-Preserving Temporal Reduction (STR) that enables existing Maximum Inner Product Search indexes to directly support TDVS. We further present Chronos, a TDVS-native framework that derives an exact metric formulation and introduces Query-Orthogonal TimeLift to control data--data geometry while preserving all query--data scores and rankings. Building on Chronos, we propose TANGO, a hierarchical graph index that adopts layer-specific TimeLift geometries to preserve temporal locality at the base layer while strengthening long-range semantic connectivity in upper layers. TANGO traverses the hierarchy using the exact TDVS score, caches temporal factors to reduce computation, and supports efficient online insertion. Extensive experiments show that TANGO achieves up to 3.5$\times$ higher query throughput and 4.05$\times$ faster index construction than state-of-the-art graph-based competitors. TANGO also maintains its advantage over all competitors across diverse temporal settings and enables efficient online insertion, demonstrating its robustness and practicality.

</details>


### [128] [Efficient discovery of unique column combinations on disk-resident data with limited memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00783)
*Xiaolong Wan,Xixian Han*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DUD的新型算法，用于在内存受限的磁盘驻留数据上高效发现唯一列组合（UCCs），通过部分差集生成和最小击中集枚举来降低计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的UCC发现算法无法很好地处理大规模磁盘驻留数据，主要受限于高内存消耗和计算成本。需要一种能够利用有限内存高效处理磁盘驻留数据的新方法。

Method: 受UCC发现与横向超图之间关系的启发，DUD算法不再生成完整的二次复杂度差集，而是仅生成部分差集用于超图构建，然后通过最小击中集枚举生成候选并进行验证。算法设计了基于选定属性上具有相同值的元组对进行两两比较来生成完整有用的差集；提出了一个新定理，可以直接报告包含所选属性的候选为真实UCC而无需验证；同时设计了基于哈希的批处理验证策略，每次只需在内存中维护少量元组。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，DUD能够在磁盘驻留数据上以高效的方式和低内存消耗发现UCCs。

Conclusion: DUD算法成功解决了大规模磁盘驻留数据上UCC发现的内存与效率瓶颈问题，为大数据环境下的数据剖析提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: The discovery of unique column combinations (UCCs) is a core task in data profiling, describing the key constraints of a table. The existing algorithms cannot deal with large-scale disk-resident data well due to high memory consumption and computational cost. In this paper, a novel DUD algorithm is developed to efficiently discover UCCs on disk-resident data with limited memory, which is inspired by the relationship between UCC discovery and transversal hypergraph. Rather than complete difference set generation of quadratic complexity, DUD only generates partial difference sets for hypergraph construction, followed by minimal hitting set enumeration to generate candidates and a validation process. DUD devises a strategy to generate full useful difference sets by pairwise comparisons of tuples having the same values with respect to some selected attributes. A novel theorem is developed and proved in this paper to report the candidates including the selected attributes as true UCCs directly without validation, which reduces the number of candidates to be validated significantly. A hash-based batch validation strategy is devised to validate a set of candidates on the relation instance, which only needs to maintain a small number of tuples in memory at a time. The extensive experimental results, conducted on synthetic and real-life data sets, show that DUD can discover UCCs on disk-resident data with high efficiency and low memory consumption.

</details>


### [129] [Relational-Core Graph Analytics Querying graphs at SQL scale, and why the node/edge model is a performance tax, not a truer picture of connected data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01525)
*Gene Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出ClickGraph和DeltaGraph系统，将Cypher图查询语言直接翻译到现有关系型数据库（ClickHouse、Databricks）及lakehouse文件的原生模式上执行，无需导入数据或单独集群。作者认为列式关系引擎在分析型图查询上不输甚至超越原生图引擎（如Neo4j可快2-4个数量级），并能扩展到内存图引擎失效的规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"图分析必须使用专用图引擎"的传统假设，指出企业实际运行的分析型工作负载中，关系型系统并非不适合连接数据。同时认为属性图并非比关系表更忠实的连接数据模型，关系的重建是纯开销。

Method: 设计ClickGraph/DeltaGraph系统，将Cypher查询直接翻译为SQL，在ClickHouse、Databricks或进程内lakehouse文件上原位执行，利用现有表、列和外键。将图查询编译为普通SQL从而保留优化空间，可重写和扩展引擎。

Result: 在同行系统自己发布的基准中，列式引擎比Neo4j快2-4个数量级；在LDBC社交网络基准套件上提供了可复现的测量结果。

Conclusion: 对实际企业工作负载而言，列式关系引擎加图查询语言前端在分析型图查询上可匹配或超越原生图引擎，且能扩展到内存图引擎无法支撑的规模。关系表中原有的外键关系无需重建，免去数据导入和独立集群的负担。

Abstract: A durable assumption holds that graph analytics requires a purpose-built graph engine, and that relational systems are ill-suited to connected data. We argue the opposite for the workloads enterprises actually run. A columnar relational engine fronted by a graph query language matches or exceeds native graph engines on analytical graph queries, and - decisively - scales past the point where in-memory graph engines fail. We further argue that the node/edge property graph is not a more faithful model of connected data but a re-encoding of relationships that already exist explicitly in relational tables; reconstructing them at query time is pure overhead. We present ClickGraph and its Databricks-dialect sibling DeltaGraph, systems that translate Cypher directly onto the native relational schema - the tables, columns, and foreign keys as they already exist - and execute in place on ClickHouse, Databricks, or in-process on lakehouse files, with no import and no separate cluster. Because the output is ordinary SQL, an underperforming query is an open optimization surface: it can be rewritten, and the engine itself extended. We support the argument with a peer system's own published benchmark, in which a columnar engine outruns Neo4j by two-to-four orders of magnitude, and with reproducible measurements across the LDBC Social Network Benchmark suite.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Harness Engineering: Anatomy, Architecture, and Evolution of Coding Agents -- A Source-Code Study of Eleven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00006)
*Paul Barbaste,Tristan Darrigol,Germain Vu,Tom Wiltberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对十一个生产级编码harness（Claude Code、Codex CLI等）及首个元harness Omnigent进行源码级解剖，定义了harness工程学科，识别出七大规范子系统、29个重复设计模式及13项横切观察，论证2026年上半年编码harness正从工具演变为平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness工程作为2026年初命名的新兴学科缺乏系统性实证基础，作者旨在通过大规模源码分析为该学科提供最全面的经验基础，厘清harness的定义、子系统架构、设计模式及其演进趋势。

Method: 对11个生产编码harness及Omnigent元harness进行源码解剖；定义七大规范子系统并映射各系统实现；结合纵向季度源码diff对照；跨越约四百万行Python/TypeScript/Rust代码进行三类语料扩展审计，提取设计模式与横切观察。

Result: 识别出13项横切观察和29个设计模式目录；发现无任何agent运行时导入通用agent框架或使用向量嵌入检索代码；SKILL.md（9/11）超越MCP（8/11）使用率；ACP在6个系统中出现并提供第三个角色"harness承载"；纵向对照显示收敛演变为模仿，行为策略从提示文本迁移至配置。

Conclusion: 编码harness在2026年上半年完成了从工具到平台的转变；论文提出18条设计建议及90行最小可行harness脚手架，为该新兴工程学科奠定经验基础。

Abstract: An agent is a model plus a harness -- the runtime that couples an LLM to the world through a loop, tools, context management, safety controls, orchestration, and extension surfaces. Harness engineering, named as a discipline in early 2026, is the design and evolution of that runtime. This paper gives the young discipline its most comprehensive empirical foundation to date: a source-code anatomy of eleven production coding harnesses (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI, Mistral Vibe, OpenHands, Aider, Mini-SWE-Agent, Hermes, Pi, OpenCode, OpenClaw), plus Omnigent, the first meta-harness, analyzed as a contrast point. We define what a harness is, map its seven canonical subsystems with the minimal and maximal implementation of each, and dissect all eleven systems along those subsystems. The audit yields 13 cross-cutting observations and a catalog of 29 recurring design patterns. Two absences survive a threefold corpus expansion: across roughly four million lines of Python, TypeScript, and Rust, no agent runtime imports a general-purpose agentic framework, and none retrieves code with vector embeddings; the field runs on hand-rolled async loops and deterministic retrieval. SKILL.md skills lead MCP in adoption (9/11 vs. 8/11), and ACP ships in six systems with a new third role: harness hosting. Because the original eight systems were re-pinned rather than replaced, the study also contains a controlled longitudinal sample -- the same harnesses source-diffed across one quarter -- showing convergence becoming imitation and behavioral policy migrating from prompt prose to configuration. These threads converge on the paper's thesis: in the first half of 2026 the coding harness completed a turn from tool to platform. The paper closes with 18 design recommendations and a 90-line minimum-viable-harness scaffold.

</details>


### [131] [Structure-Behavior Coalescence and the Limits of Traditional Systems Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00042)
*William S. Chao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文挑战传统系统理论中结构与行为可分离的基本假设，提出"结构-行为融合"（SBC）原则，主张二者是相互构成的单一系统过程，系统身份源于结构与行为的持续共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统理论将结构（组成部分的组织）与行为（系统活动随时间的演化）视为可分离的分析维度，这种分离导致在解释系统身份（尤其是涉及变化、涌现和边界界定）时存在持续困难。

Method: 提出结构-行为融合（SBC）作为重构原则，将结构与行为重新概念化为单一系统过程中相互构成的方面，而非独立存在、通过建模构造才关联的实体。

Result: 提供了一种统一的方式来理解系统身份、涌现和边界形成，从控制论视角下的系统观点出发，将系统身份视为结构与行为持续共同决定的产物。

Conclusion: 系统身份来自结构组织与行为动力的持续共同决定，而非它们之间的外部对应或对齐，这一重构为系统理论中身份、涌现和边界的理解提供了更一致的框架。

Abstract: This paper examines a foundational assumption in traditional systems theory, namely that structure (the organization of components) and behavior (the evolution of system activity over time) can be treated as separable analytical dimensions. It argues that this separation contributes to persistent difficulties in explaining system identity, particularly in cases involving change, emergence, and boundary specification. To address this issue, the paper introduces Structure-Behavior Coalescence (SBC) as a reframing principle. SBC proposes that structure and behavior should not be understood as independently existing entities that are subsequently related through modeling constructs, but as mutually constitutive aspects of a single systemic process. From this perspective, system identity is understood as arising from the sustained co-determination of structural organization and behavioral dynamics, rather than from their external correspondence or alignment. This reframing provides a unified way of understanding system identity, emergence, and boundary formation within a cybernetically informed systems perspective.

</details>


### [132] [What Is a System? An Interaction-Based Account of Structure-Behavior Coalescence in General Systems Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00043)
*William S. Chao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出结构-行为融合（SBC）作为基于交互的系统定义新视角，主张系统的结构与行为通过实体间交互不可分割地融合在一起，并探讨其对一般系统理论中系统同一性、边界、涌现等概念的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统定义多从要素、关系、边界、功能或交互等角度出发，但未能统一解释系统结构与行为如何相互构成。作者希望提出一个能够弥合结构与行为二元分裂的统一系统观。

Method: 提出"结构-行为融合"（Structure-Behavior Coalescence, SBC）概念框架，区分系统结构、交互与行为三个概念，并论证三者不可分割；将交互视为结构转化为行为以及行为展示结构的核心纽带。

Result: 论证了系统应被视为一个统一实体，其行为源于组成实体的交互，交互同时贡献于系统的结构组织与行为实现；SBC为系统同一性、边界、行为涌现及复杂系统表征提供了新的理论基础。

Conclusion: SBC作为一般系统理论的补充性概念基础，将结构与行为的交互融合置于系统定义的核心，强调系统"是什么"（结构）与系统"做什么"（行为）之间的一致性。

Abstract: The question of what constitutes a system remains fundamental to General Systems Theory. Existing definitions commonly characterize a system in terms of elements, relationships, boundaries, functions, or interactions, but these perspectives do not always provide a unified account of how system structure and system behavior constitute one another. This paper proposes Structure-Behavior Coalescence (SBC) as an interaction-based account of what a system is. From the SBC perspective, a system is not merely a collection of elements or relationships, nor is it adequately characterized by behavior considered independently of structure. Rather, a system is a structured entity whose behavior arises through interactions among its constituent entities, with those interactions simultaneously contributing to both its structural organization and behavioral realization. The paper develops this perspective by distinguishing system structure, interaction, and behavior while treating them as inseparable aspects of a unified system representation. It argues that interactions provide the essential link through which structural relationships become behavioral processes and through which behavioral processes reveal and instantiate system structure. This perspective provides a basis for representing systems in a manner that maintains consistency between what a system is and what a system does. The paper further considers the implications of SBC for General Systems Theory, particularly for system identity, system boundaries, behavioral emergence, and the representation of complex systems. SBC is presented as a general conceptual foundation that complements existing systems theories by placing the coalescence of structure and behavior through interaction at the center of the definition of a system.

</details>


### [133] [Towards Agentic Cloud Engineering: Graph and Loop Engineering with a Zero-Trust Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00050)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic Cloud Workflow Engineering框架，将自然语言的智能体云工程任务转化为经过验证的代码仓库和可验证的云部署，通过图工程、循环工程和智能体封装工程三个互补关注点实现长时程工作流的稳健执行与有界恢复，并在Google Cloud上完成实例化与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI驱动的基于云的工作流需要显式机制来保障工作流推进、约束执行、故障恢复和可验证完成，而现有方法缺乏统一的工程化架构来处理这些需求。

Method: 该框架分离三个互补关注点：图工程（graph engineering）规范长时程工作流推进及依赖验证的状态转换；循环工程（loop engineering）提供有界诊断、修复或重规划、重试与重新验证；智能体封装工程（agent harness engineering）通过身份、授权、策略作用域能力、隔离和运行时防护强制零信任执行。

Result: 在Google Cloud上的实验表明，执行能够在有界恢复条件下以两种结果终止：得到验证的可操作云部署，或可审计的终止性失败。框架验证了仓库完整性、受控执行、证据门控推进、可操作部署和有界恢复能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为涵盖Agentic DevOps、CloudOps、SRE/AIOps、SecOps、DataOps、MLOps/LLMOps、AgentOps、RAG/GraphRAG等领域的基于云的智能体工作流提供了统一的工程架构。

Abstract: Agentic AI is enabling cloud-based workflows in which autonomous agents reason over operational state, invoke authorized tools, modify software and infrastructure, deploy services, verify execution outcomes, and adapt across long-horizon, multistep tasks. Engineering such workflows requires explicit mechanisms for workflow progression, constrained execution, failure recovery, and verifiable completion.
